```
    63→def _resolve_import_payload(

    65→    *,

    95→def _build_working_state(state: dict, state_file) -> dict:
    96→    """Return state snapshot used for import mutation/dry-run rendering."""
    97→    state_path = Path(state_file) if state_file is not None else None
    98→    if state_path is not None and state_path.exists():
    99→        return copy.deepcopy(state_mod.load_state(state_path))
   100→    return copy.deepcopy(state)
   101→
   102→
   103→def _apply_assessment_policy(
   104→    *,
   105→    working_state: dict,
   106→    issues_data: dict,
   107→    assessment_policy: AssessmentImportPolicyModel,
   108→) -> int:
   109→    """Apply provisional/clear flags based on assessment policy mode."""
   110→    assessment_keys = imported_assessment_keys(issues_data)
   111→    if assessment_policy.mode == "manual_override":
   112→        return mark_manual_override_assessments_provisional(
   113→            working_state,
   114→            assessment_keys=assessment_keys,
   115→        )
   116→    if assessment_policy.mode in {"trusted_internal", "attested_external"}:
   117→        clear_provisional_override_flags(
   118→            working_state,
   119→            assessment_keys=assessment_keys,
   120→        )
   121→    return 0
   122→
   123→
   124→def _raise_on_partial_skip(diff: dict, *, allow_partial: bool) -> None:
   125→    """Refuse import when payload skips issues and partial imports are disabled."""
   126→    if diff.get("skipped", 0) <= 0 or allow_partial:
   127→        return
   128→    details_lines: list[str] = []
   129→    for detail in diff.get("skipped_details", []):
   130→        reasons = "; ".join(detail.get("missing", []))
   131→        details_lines.append(
   132→            f"  #{detail.get('index', '?')} ({detail.get('identifier', '<none>')}): {reasons}"
   133→        )
   134→    msg = "import produced skipped issue(s); refusing partial import."
   135→    if details_lines:
   136→        msg += "\n" + "\n".join(details_lines)
   137→    msg += "\nFix the payload and retry, or pass --allow-partial to override."
   138→    raise CommandError(msg, exit_code=1)
   139→
   140→
   141→def _append_assessment_import_audit(
   142→    *,
   143→    working_state: dict,
   144→    assessment_policy: AssessmentImportPolicyModel,
   145→    provisional_count: int,
   146→    override_attest: str | None,
   147→    import_file,
   148→) -> None:
   149→    """Record audit metadata for assessment-bearing import payloads."""
   150→    if not assessment_policy.assessments_present:
   151→        return
   152→    audit = working_state.setdefault("assessment_import_audit", [])
   153→    audit.append(
   154→        {
   155→            "timestamp": state_mod.utc_now(),
   156→            "mode": assessment_policy.mode,
   157→            "trusted": bool(assessment_policy.trusted),
   158→            "reason": assessment_policy.reason,
   159→            "override_used": bool(assessment_policy.mode == "manual_override"),
   160→            "attested_external": bool(assessment_policy.mode == "attested_external"),
   161→            "provisional": bool(assessment_policy.mode == "manual_override"),
   162→            "provisional_count": int(provisional_count),
   163→            "attest": (override_attest or "").strip(),
   164→            "import_file": str(import_file),
   165→        }
   166→    )
   167→
   168→
   169→def _persist_import_state(
   170→    *,
   171→    state: dict,
   172→    working_state: dict,
   173→    state_file,
   174→    diff: dict,
   175→    assessment_mode: str,
   176→    config: dict | None,
   177→    import_file: str,
   178→    import_payload: dict,
   179→) -> None:
   180→    """Persist imported state and synchronize the work plan."""
   181→    state.clear()
   182→    state.update(working_state)
   183→    state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
   184→    sync_plan_after_import(
   185→        state,
   186→        diff,
   187→        assessment_mode,
   188→        request=PlanImportSyncRequest(
   189→            state_file=state_file,
   190→            config=config,
   191→            import_file=import_file,
   192→            import_payload=import_payload,
   193→        ),
   194→    )
   195→
   196→
   197→def _guard_pending_import_scores_match(
   198→    *,
   199→    state: dict,
   200→    state_file,
   201→    import_file: str,
   202→    issues_data: dict,
   203→    assessment_policy: AssessmentImportPolicyModel,
   204→) -> None:
   205→    """Refuse durable imports that do not match the queued score-import batch."""
   206→    if assessment_policy.mode not in {"trusted_internal", "attested_external"}:
   207→        return
   208→    plan_path = plan_path_for_state(Path(state_file))
   209→    if not has_living_plan(plan_path):
   210→        return
   211→    plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   212→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   213→        return
   214→    pending_meta = pending_import_scores_meta(plan, state)
   215→    matches, reason = import_scores_meta_matches(
   216→        pending_meta,
   217→        import_file=import_file,
   218→        import_payload=issues_data,
   219→    )
   220→    if matches:
   221→        return
   222→    expected_file = ""
   223→    if pending_meta is not None:
   224→        expected_file = pending_meta.import_file.strip()
   225→    raise CommandError(
   226→        "Refusing durable score import: the pending "
   227→        "`workflow::import-scores` task is bound to a different review batch.\n"
   228→        f"  - {reason}\n"
   229→        + (f"Expected queued import file: {expected_file}\n" if expected_file else "")
   230→        + "Use the exact file shown by `desloppify next`, or clear the stale workflow item first.",
   231→        exit_code=1,
   232→    )
   233→
   234→
   235→def _has_refreshable_scorecard_context(state: dict) -> bool:
   236→    """Return True when state has scan-backed scorecard context.
   237→
   238→    Review imports can run against minimal/synthetic states (for example test
   239→    fixtures or pre-scan workflows). Refreshing the badge from those states can
   240→    overwrite scorecard.png with a misleading partial card.
   241→    """
   242→    if not state.get("last_scan"):
   243→        return False
   244→
   245→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores")
   246→    if not isinstance(dim_scores, dict) or not dim_scores:
   247→        return False
   248→
   249→    for data in dim_scores.values():
   250→        if not isinstance(data, dict):
   251→            continue
   252→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
   253→        if not isinstance(detectors, dict):
   254→            continue
   255→        if "subjective_assessment" in detectors:
   256→            continue
   257→        if int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0) <= 0:
   258→            continue
   259→        return True
   260→    return False
   261→
   262→
   263→def _refresh_scorecard_after_import(
   264→    *,
   265→    state: dict,
   266→    config: dict | None,
   267→    assessment_policy: AssessmentImportPolicyModel,
   268→) -> bool:
   269→    """Refresh the scorecard badge when a trusted import updates live scores."""
   270→    if not assessment_policy.assessments_present or not assessment_policy.trusted:
   271→        return False
   272→    if not _has_refreshable_scorecard_context(state):
   273→        return False
   274→    emit_scorecard_badge(
   275→        SimpleNamespace(no_badge=False, badge_path=None),
   276→        config or {},
   277→        state,
   278→    )
   279→    return True
   280→
   281→
   282→def do_import(
   283→    import_file,
   284→    state,
   285→    lang,
   286→    state_file,
   287→    *,
   288→    import_config: ReviewImportConfig | None = None,
   289→    dry_run: bool = False,
   290→) -> None:
   291→    """Import mode: ingest agent-produced issues."""
   292→    resolved_import_config = import_config or ReviewImportConfig()
   293→    issues_data, _override_enabled, override_attest = _resolve_import_payload(
   294→        import_file,
   295→        lang_name=lang.name,
   296→        import_config=resolved_import_config,
   297→    )
   298→
   299→    assessment_policy: AssessmentImportPolicyModel = (
   300→        assessment_policy_model_from_payload(issues_data)
   301→    )
   302→    print_assessment_mode_banner(
   303→        assessment_policy.to_dict(),
   304→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   305→    )
   306→    print_assessment_policy_notice(
   307→        assessment_policy.to_dict(),
   308→        import_file=str(import_file),
   309→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   310→    )
   311→    _guard_pending_import_scores_match(
   312→        state=state,
   313→        state_file=state_file,
   314→        import_file=str(import_file),
   315→        issues_data=issues_data,
   316→        assessment_policy=assessment_policy,
   317→    )
   318→
   319→    prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   320→    working_state = _build_working_state(state, state_file)
   321→
   322→    diff = import_holistic_issues(issues_data, working_state, lang.name)
   323→    label = "Holistic review"
   324→    provisional_count = _apply_assessment_policy(
   325→        working_state=working_state,
   326→        issues_data=issues_data,
   327→        assessment_policy=assessment_policy,
   328→    )
   329→    _raise_on_partial_skip(diff, allow_partial=resolved_import_config.allow_partial)
   330→    _append_assessment_import_audit(
   331→        working_state=working_state,
   332→        assessment_policy=assessment_policy,
   333→        provisional_count=provisional_count,
   334→        override_attest=override_attest,

   337→
   338→    if not dry_run:

   340→            state=state,

   342→            state_file=state_file,
   343→            diff=diff,
   344→            assessment_mode=assessment_policy.mode,
   345→            config=resolved_import_config.config,
   346→            import_file=str(import_file),
   347→            import_payload=issues_data,
   348→        )
   349→
   350→    display_state = state if not dry_run else working_state
   351→    report_review_import_outcome(
   352→        state=display_state,
   353→        lang_name=lang.name,
   354→        config=resolved_import_config.config,
   355→        diff=diff,

   359→        assessment_policy=assessment_policy,
   360→        scorecard_subjective_at_target_fn=_SCORECARD_SUBJECTIVE_AT_TARGET,
   361→    )
   362→    if not dry_run:
```

```
1→"""Result rendering helpers for review import flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

     6→from desloppify import state_compat as state_compat
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import show_score_with_plan_context
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.reporting import dimensions as reporting_dimensions
    10→from desloppify.base.config import target_strict_score_from_config
    11→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    12→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative.core import NarrativeContext
    13→
    14→from .output import (
    15→    print_assessments_summary,
    16→    print_open_review_summary,
    17→    print_review_import_scores_and_integrity,
    18→    print_skipped_validation_details,
    19→)
    20→
    21→
    22→def report_review_import_outcome(
    23→    *,
    24→    state: dict,
    25→    lang_name: str,
    26→    config: dict | None,
    27→    diff: dict,
    28→    prev,
    29→    label: str,
    30→    provisional_count: int,
    31→    assessment_policy,
    32→    scorecard_subjective_at_target_fn,
    33→) -> None:
    34→    """Render review import output and refresh query.json."""
    35→    narrative = narrative_core.compute_narrative(
    36→        state,
    37→        NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="review"),
    38→    )
    39→
    40→    print(colorize(f"\n  {label} imported:", "bold"))
    41→    issue_count = int(diff.get("new", 0) or 0)
    42→    print(
    43→        colorize(
    44→            f"  +{issue_count} new issue{'s' if issue_count != 1 else ''} "
    45→            f"(review issues), "
    46→            f"{diff['auto_resolved']} resolved, "
    47→            f"{diff['reopened']} reopened",
    48→            "dim",
    49→        )
    50→    )
    51→    if provisional_count > 0:
    52→        print(
    53→            colorize(
    54→                "  WARNING: manual override assessments are provisional and will "
    55→                "reset on the next scan unless replaced by "
    56→                "a trusted review path (see skill doc for options).",
    57→                "yellow",
    58→            )
    59→        )
    60→    print_skipped_validation_details(diff, colorize_fn=colorize)
    61→    print_assessments_summary(state, colorize_fn=colorize)
    62→    next_command = print_open_review_summary(
    63→        state,
    64→        colorize_fn=colorize,
    65→    )
    66→    show_score_with_plan_context(state, prev)
    67→    at_target = print_review_import_scores_and_integrity(
    68→        state,
    69→        config or {},
    70→        state_mod=state_compat,
    71→        target_strict_score_from_config_fn=target_strict_score_from_config,
    72→        subjective_at_target_fn=scorecard_subjective_at_target_fn,
    73→        subjective_rerun_command_fn=reporting_dimensions.subjective_rerun_command,
    74→        colorize_fn=colorize,
    75→    )
    76→
    77→    print(
    78→        colorize(
    79→            f"  Next command to improve subjective scores: `{next_command}`",
    80→            "dim",
    81→        )
    82→    )
    83→    write_query(
    84→        {
    85→            "command": "review",
    86→            "action": "import",
    87→            "mode": "holistic",
    88→            "diff": diff,
    89→            "next_command": next_command,
    90→            "subjective_at_target": [
    91→                {"dimension": entry["name"], "score": entry["score"]}
    92→                for entry in at_target

    94→            "assessment_import": {

    96→                "trusted": bool(assessment_policy.trusted),
    97→                "reason": assessment_policy.reason,
    98→            },
    99→            "narrative": narrative,
   100→        }
   101→    )
```

```
1→"""State/config persistence boundaries for command handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state_compat as state_compat
     8→from desloppify.base import config as config_mod
     9→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import CommandError
    10→
    11→
    12→def save_state_or_exit(state: dict, state_file: Path | None) -> None:
    13→    """Persist state with a consistent CLI error boundary."""
    14→    try:
    15→        state_compat.save_state(state, state_file)
    16→    except OSError as exc:
    17→        raise CommandError(f"could not save state: {exc}") from exc
    18→
    19→
    20→def save_config_or_exit(config: dict) -> None:
    21→    """Persist config with a consistent CLI error boundary."""
    22→    try:
    23→        config_mod.save_config(config)
    24→    except OSError as exc:
    25→        raise CommandError(f"could not save config: {exc}") from exc
    26→
    27→
    28→__all__ = ["save_config_or_exit", "save_state_or_exit"]
    29→
```

```
    57→    "get_state_file",
    58→    "CURRENT_VERSION",
    59→    "utc_now",
    60→    "empty_state",
    61→    "ensure_state_defaults",
    62→    "scan_source",
    63→    "scan_metadata",
    64→    "scan_inventory_available",
    65→    "scan_metrics_available",
    66→    "scan_reconstructed_issue_count",
    67→    "validate_state_invariants",
    68→    "json_default",
    69→    "migrate_state_keys",
    70→]
    71→
    72→_ALLOWED_ISSUE_STATUSES: set[str] = {
    73→    *issue_status_tokens(),
    74→}
    75→_SCAN_METADATA_SOURCES = {"empty", "scan", "plan_reconstruction"}
    76→
    77→
    78→def get_state_dir() -> Path:
    79→    """Return the active state directory for the current runtime context."""
    80→    return get_project_root() / ".desloppify"
    81→
    82→
    83→def get_state_file() -> Path:
    84→    """Return the default state file for the current runtime context."""
    85→    return get_state_dir() / "state.json"
    86→
    87→
    88→CURRENT_VERSION = 1
    89→
    90→
    91→def utc_now() -> str:
    92→    """Return current UTC timestamp with second-level precision."""
    93→    return datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
    94→
    95→
    96→def empty_state() -> StateModel:
    97→    """Return a new empty state payload."""
    98→    return {
    99→        "version": CURRENT_VERSION,
   100→        "created": utc_now(),
   101→        "last_scan": None,
   102→        "scan_count": 0,
   103→        "overall_score": 0,
   104→        "objective_score": 0,
   105→        "strict_score": 0,
   106→        "verified_strict_score": 0,
   107→        "stats": {},
   108→        "issues": {},
   109→        "scan_coverage": {},
   110→        "score_confidence": {},
   111→        "scan_metadata": {"source": "empty"},
   112→        "subjective_integrity": {},
   113→        "subjective_assessments": {},
   114→    }
   115→
   116→
   117→def _as_non_negative_int(value: Any, default: int = 0) -> int:
   118→    try:
   119→        parsed = int(value)
   120→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   121→        return default
   122→    return parsed if parsed >= 0 else 0
   123→
   124→
   125→def _rename_key(d: dict, old: str, new: str) -> bool:
   126→    if old not in d:
   127→        return False
   128→    d.setdefault(new, d.pop(old))
   129→    return True
   130→
   131→
   132→def migrate_state_keys(state: StateModel | dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   133→    """Migrate legacy key names in-place.
   134→
   135→    - ``"findings"`` → ``"issues"``
   136→    - ``dimension_scores[dim]["issues"]`` → ``"failing"``
   137→    """
   138→    state_dict = cast(dict[str, Any], state)
   139→    _rename_key(state_dict, "findings", "issues")
   140→
   141→    for ds in state_dict.get("dimension_scores", {}).values():
   142→        if isinstance(ds, dict):
   143→            _rename_key(ds, "issues", "failing")
   144→
   145→    for entry in state_dict.get("scan_history", []):
   146→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   147→            continue
   148→        _rename_key(entry, "raw_findings", "raw_issues")
   149→        for ds in (entry.get("dimension_scores") or {}).values():
   150→            if isinstance(ds, dict):
   151→                _rename_key(ds, "issues", "failing")
   152→
   153→
   154→def _normalize_scan_metadata(state: StateModel | dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   155→    raw_metadata = state.get("scan_metadata")
   156→    metadata = raw_metadata if isinstance(raw_metadata, dict) else {}
   157→
   158→    source = _coerce_scan_source(state, metadata)
   159→    normalized: ScanMetadataModel = {"source": source}
   160→    if source == "plan_reconstruction":
   161→        normalized["plan_queue_available"] = bool(metadata.get("plan_queue_available"))
   162→        issue_count = metadata.get("reconstructed_issue_count", 0)
   163→        if isinstance(issue_count, int) and not isinstance(issue_count, bool):
   164→            normalized["reconstructed_issue_count"] = max(0, issue_count)
   165→        else:
   166→            normalized["reconstructed_issue_count"] = 0
   167→
   168→    state["scan_metadata"] = normalized
   169→
   170→
   171→def ensure_state_defaults(state: StateModel | dict) -> None:
   172→    """Normalize loose/legacy state payloads to a valid base shape in-place."""
   173→    migrate_state_keys(state)
   174→
   175→    mutable_state = cast(dict[str, Any], state)
   176→    for key, value in empty_state().items():
   177→        mutable_state.setdefault(key, value)
   178→
   179→    if not isinstance(state.get("issues"), dict):
   180→        state["issues"] = {}
   181→    if not isinstance(state.get("stats"), dict):
   182→        state["stats"] = {}
   183→    if not isinstance(state.get("scan_history"), list):
   184→        state["scan_history"] = []
   185→    if not isinstance(state.get("scan_coverage"), dict):
   186→        state["scan_coverage"] = {}
   187→    if not isinstance(state.get("score_confidence"), dict):
   188→        state["score_confidence"] = {}
   189→    if not isinstance(state.get("subjective_integrity"), dict):
   190→        state["subjective_integrity"] = {}
   191→    _normalize_scan_metadata(state)
   192→
   193→    all_issues = state["issues"]
   194→    to_remove: list[str] = []
   195→    for issue_id, issue in all_issues.items():
   196→        if not isinstance(issue, dict):
   197→            to_remove.append(issue_id)
   198→            continue
   199→
   200→        issue.setdefault("id", issue_id)
```

```
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse

    10→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    11→
    12→from ..services import TriageServices

    15→
    16→
    17→def is_full_stage_run(stages_to_run: list[str]) -> bool:
    18→    """True when the pipeline was asked to run the full triage stage set."""
    19→    return set(stages_to_run) == set(STAGES)
    20→
    21→
    22→def all_stage_results_successful(
    23→    *,
    24→    stages_to_run: list[str],
    25→    stage_results: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Any]],
    26→) -> bool:
    27→    """True when each requested stage is confirmed or already confirmed."""
    28→    for stage in stages_to_run:
    29→        status = str(stage_results.get(stage, {}).get("status", ""))
    30→        if status not in {"confirmed", "skipped"}:
    31→            return False
    32→    return True
    33→
    34→
    35→def print_not_finalized_message(reason: str) -> None:
    36→    """Emit a consistent next-step message when auto-completion is skipped/blocked."""
    37→    print(colorize(f"\n  Stages complete, triage not finalized ({reason}).", "yellow"))
    38→    print(
    39→        colorize(
    40→            '  Finalize manually: desloppify plan triage --complete --strategy "<execution plan>"',
    41→            "dim",
    42→        )
    43→    )
    44→
    45→
    46→def validate_and_confirm_stage(
    47→    *,
    48→    stage: str,
    49→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    50→    services: TriageServices,
    51→    triage_input: Any,
    52→    state: Any,
    53→    repo_root,
    54→    stage_start: float,
    55→    append_run_log,
    56→) -> tuple[bool, dict, str]:
    57→    """Run shared stage validation + confirmation flow."""
    58→    plan = services.load_plan()
    59→
    60→    ok, error_msg = validate_stage(
    61→        stage,
    62→        plan,
    63→        state,
    64→        repo_root,
    65→        triage_input=triage_input,
    66→    )
    67→    if not ok:
    68→        elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - stage_start)
    69→        print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: validation failed: {error_msg}", "red"))
    70→        print(colorize("  Re-run to resume.", "dim"))
    71→        append_run_log(
    72→            f"stage-validation-failed stage={stage} elapsed={elapsed}s error={error_msg}"
    73→        )
    74→        return (
    75→            False,
    76→            {
    77→                "status": "validation_failed",
    78→                "elapsed_seconds": elapsed,
    79→                "error": error_msg,
    80→            },
    81→            "",
    82→        )
    83→
    84→    attestation = build_auto_attestation(stage, plan, triage_input)
    85→    confirm_args = argparse.Namespace(
    86→        confirm=stage,
    87→        attestation=attestation,
    88→        state=getattr(args, "state", None),
    89→    )
    90→
    91→    from ..confirmations.router import cmd_confirm_stage
    92→
    93→    cmd_confirm_stage(confirm_args, services=services)
    94→
    95→    plan = services.load_plan()
    96→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    97→    stages_data = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
    98→    elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - stage_start)
    99→    if stage in stages_data and stages_data[stage].get("confirmed_at"):
   100→        print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: confirmed ({elapsed}s).", "green"))
   101→        append_run_log(f"stage-confirmed stage={stage} elapsed={elapsed}s")
   102→        report = stages_data.get(stage, {}).get("report", "")
   103→        return (
   104→            True,
   105→            {"status": "confirmed", "elapsed_seconds": elapsed},
   106→            report,
   107→        )
   108→
   109→    print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: auto-confirmation did not take effect.", "red"))
   110→    print(colorize("  Re-run to resume.", "dim"))
   111→    append_run_log(f"stage-confirm-failed stage={stage} elapsed={elapsed}s")
   112→    return (
   113→        False,
   114→        {"status": "confirm_failed", "elapsed_seconds": elapsed},
   115→        "",
   116→    )
   117→
   118→
   119→def build_completion_strategy(stages_data: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str:
   120→    """Derive a completion strategy from stage reports."""
   121→    strategy_parts: list[str] = []
   122→    for stage in STAGES:
   123→        report = str(stages_data.get(stage, {}).get("report", ""))
   124→        if report:
   125→            strategy_parts.append(f"[{stage}] {report[:200]}")
   126→    strategy = " ".join(strategy_parts)
   127→    if len(strategy) < 200:
   128→        strategy = strategy + " " + "Automated triage via codex subagent pipeline. " * 3
   129→    return strategy
   130→
   131→
   132→def complete_pipeline(
   133→    *,
   134→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   135→    services: TriageServices,
   136→    plan: Mapping[str, Any],
   137→    strategy: str,
   138→    triage_input: Any,
   139→) -> bool:
   140→    """Run the triage completion coordinator and report success."""
   141→    completed_before = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("last_completed_at")
   142→
   143→    print(colorize("\n  Completing triage...", "bold"))
   144→
   145→    attestation = build_auto_attestation("sense-check", plan, triage_input)
   146→    complete_args = argparse.Namespace(
   147→        complete=True,
   148→        strategy=strategy[:2000],
   149→        attestation=attestation,
   150→        state=getattr(args, "state", None),
   151→    )
   152→
   153→    from ..stages.completion import _cmd_triage_complete
   154→
   155→    _cmd_triage_complete(complete_args, services=services)
   156→
   157→    completed_after = (
   158→        services.load_plan().get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("last_completed_at")
   159→    )
   160→    return bool(completed_after and completed_after != completed_before)
   161→
   162→

   168→    "print_not_finalized_message",
   169→    "validate_and_confirm_stage",
   170→]
```

```
1→"""Observe stage command flow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     8→from desloppify.base.output.user_message import print_user_message
     9→
    10→from ..stage_queue import (
    11→    cascade_clear_dispositions,
    12→    has_triage_in_queue,
    13→    inject_triage_stages,
    14→    print_cascade_clear_feedback,
    15→)
    16→from ..lifecycle import TriageLifecycleDeps, ensure_triage_started
    17→from ..services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    18→from .flow_helpers import validate_stage_report_length
    19→from .records import record_observe_stage, resolve_reusable_report
    20→from .rendering import _print_observe_report_requirement
    21→
    22→
    23→def cmd_stage_observe(
    24→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    25→    *,
    26→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
    27→    has_triage_in_queue_fn=has_triage_in_queue,
    28→    inject_triage_stages_fn=inject_triage_stages,
    29→) -> None:
    30→    """Record the OBSERVE stage: agent analyses themes and root causes."""
    31→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
    32→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
    33→
    34→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
    35→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
    36→    state = runtime.state
    37→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
    38→
    39→    if not has_triage_in_queue_fn(plan):
    40→        start_outcome = ensure_triage_started(
    41→            plan,
    42→            services=resolved_services,
    43→            state=state,
    44→            attestation=attestation,
    45→            start_message="  Planning mode auto-started (6 stages queued).",
    46→            deps=TriageLifecycleDeps(
    47→                has_triage_in_queue=has_triage_in_queue_fn,
    48→                inject_triage_stages=inject_triage_stages_fn,
    49→            ),
    50→        )
    51→        if start_outcome.status == "blocked":
    52→            return
    53→
    54→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
    55→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
    56→    existing_stage = stages.get("observe")
    57→
    58→    report, is_reuse = resolve_reusable_report(report, existing_stage)
    59→    if not report:
    60→        _print_observe_report_requirement()
    61→        return
    62→
    63→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
    64→    issue_count = len(si.open_issues)
    65→    if issue_count == 0:
    66→        cleared = record_observe_stage(
    67→            stages,
    68→            report=report,
    69→            issue_count=0,
    70→            cited_ids=[],
    71→            existing_stage=existing_stage,
    72→            is_reuse=is_reuse,
    73→        )
    74→        resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
    75→        print(colorize("  Observe stage recorded (no issues to analyse).", "green"))
    76→        if is_reuse:
    77→            print(colorize("  Observe data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
    78→            if cleared:
    79→                print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
    80→        return
    81→
    82→    if not validate_stage_report_length(
    83→        report=report,
    84→        issue_count=issue_count,
    85→        guidance="  Describe themes, root causes, contradictions, and how issues relate.",
    86→    ):
    87→        return
    88→
    89→    from .evidence_parsing import (
    90→        format_evidence_failures,
    91→        parse_observe_evidence,
    92→        resolve_short_hash_to_full_id,
    93→        validate_observe_evidence,
    94→    )
    95→
    96→    valid_ids = set(si.open_issues.keys())
    97→    cited = resolved_services.extract_issue_citations(report, valid_ids)
    98→    evidence = parse_observe_evidence(report, valid_ids)
    99→    evidence_failures = validate_observe_evidence(evidence, issue_count)
   100→    blocking = [failure for failure in evidence_failures if failure.blocking]
   101→    advisory = [failure for failure in evidence_failures if not failure.blocking]
   102→    if blocking:
   103→        print(colorize(format_evidence_failures(blocking, stage_label="observe"), "red"))
   104→        return
   105→    if advisory:
   106→        print(colorize(format_evidence_failures(advisory, stage_label="observe"), "yellow"))
   107→
   108→    assessments = [
   109→        {
   110→            "hash": entry.issue_hash,
   111→            "verdict": entry.verdict,
   112→            "verdict_reasoning": entry.verdict_reasoning,
   113→            "files_read": entry.files_read,
   114→            "recommendation": entry.recommendation,
   115→        }
   116→        for entry in evidence.entries
   117→    ]
   118→
   119→    # On fresh observe run, cascade-clear dispositions and undo auto-skips
   120→    if not is_reuse:
   121→        from ..confirmations.basic import _undo_observe_auto_skips
   122→
   123→        _undo_observe_auto_skips(plan, meta)
   124→        cascade_clear_dispositions(meta, "observe")
   125→
   126→    # Populate issue_dispositions from assessments using collision-aware resolution
   127→    dispositions: dict[str, dict] = {}
   128→    for entry in evidence.entries:
   129→        full_id = resolve_short_hash_to_full_id(entry.issue_hash, valid_ids)
   130→        if full_id:
   131→            dispositions[full_id] = {
   132→                "verdict": entry.verdict,
   133→                "verdict_reasoning": entry.verdict_reasoning,
   134→                "files_read": entry.files_read,
   135→                "recommendation": entry.recommendation,
   136→            }
   137→    meta["issue_dispositions"] = dispositions
   138→
   139→    cleared = record_observe_stage(
   140→        stages,
   141→        report=report,
   142→        issue_count=issue_count,
   143→        cited_ids=sorted(cited),
   144→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   145→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   146→        assessments=assessments,
   147→    )
   148→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   149→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   150→        plan,
   151→        "triage_observe",
   152→        actor="user",
   153→        detail={"issue_count": issue_count, "cited_ids": sorted(cited), "reuse": is_reuse},
   154→    )
   155→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   156→
   157→    print(colorize(f"  Observe stage recorded: {issue_count} issues analysed.", "green"))
   158→    if is_reuse:
   159→        print(colorize("  Observe data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   160→        if cleared:
   161→            print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   162→        return
   163→
   164→    print(colorize("  Now confirm your analysis.", "yellow"))
   165→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm observe", "dim"))
   166→    print_user_message(
   167→        "Observe recorded. Before confirming — did the subagent"
   168→        " verify every issue with code reads? Check: are there"
   169→        " specific file/line citations in the report, or just"
   170→        " restated issue titles? Each issue needs a verdict:"
   171→        " genuine / false positive / exaggerated / not-worth-it. Don't confirm"
   172→        " until the analysis is backed by actual code evidence."
   173→    )
   174→
   175→_cmd_stage_observe = cmd_stage_observe
   176→
   177→
   178→__all__ = ["_cmd_stage_observe", "cmd_stage_observe"]
   179→
```

```
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable
```

```
    10→
    11→CoverageStatus = Literal["full", "reduced"]
    12→
    13→
    14→class DetectorCoverageRecord(TypedDict, total=False):
    15→    """Persisted detector-level coverage confidence metadata."""
    16→
    17→    detector: str
    18→    status: CoverageStatus
    19→    confidence: float
    20→    summary: str
    21→    impact: str
    22→    remediation: str
    23→    tool: str

    27→class ScanCoverageRecord(TypedDict, total=False):
    28→    """Persisted scan-level coverage snapshot for one language run."""
    29→
    30→    status: CoverageStatus
    31→    confidence: float
    32→    detectors: dict[str, DetectorCoverageRecord]

    35→
    36→

    38→class DetectorCoverageStatus:

    47→    tool: str = ""
    48→    reason: str = ""
    49→
    50→

    52→class LangSecurityResult:
```

```
    27→def _merge_observe_outputs(
    28→    batch_outputs: list[tuple[list[str], Path]],
    29→) -> str:
    30→    """Concatenate batch outputs with dimension headers into single observe report."""
    31→    parts: list[str] = []
    32→    for dims, output_file in batch_outputs:
    33→        header = f"## Dimensions: {', '.join(dims)}"
    34→        content = ""

    37→                content = output_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").strip()
    38→            except OSError:
    39→                content = "(batch output missing)"
    40→        if not content:
    41→            content = "(batch produced no output)"
    42→        parts.append(f"{header}\n\n{content}")
    43→    return "\n\n---\n\n".join(parts)
    44→
    45→
    46→def run_observe(
    47→    *,
    48→    si,
    49→    repo_root: Path,
    50→    prompts_dir: Path,
    51→    output_dir: Path,
    52→    logs_dir: Path,
    53→    timeout_seconds: int,
    54→    dry_run: bool = False,
    55→    append_run_log=None,
    56→) -> TriageStageRunResult:
    57→    """Run observe stage via codex subprocess batches."""
    58→    _log = append_run_log or _noop_log
    59→
    60→    batches = group_issues_into_observe_batches(si)
    61→    total = len(batches)
    62→    print(colorize(f"\n  Observe: splitting into {total} parallel batches.", "bold"))
    63→    _log(f"observe-parallel batches={total}")
    64→
    65→    tasks: dict[int, Callable[[], TriageStageRunResult]] = {}
    66→    batch_meta: list[tuple[list[str], Path]] = []
    67→
    68→    for i, (dims, issues_subset) in enumerate(batches):
    69→        prompt = build_observe_batch_prompt(
    70→            batch_index=i + 1,
    71→            total_batches=total,
    72→            dimension_group=dims,
    73→            issues_subset=issues_subset,
    74→            repo_root=repo_root,
    75→        )
    76→        prompt_file = prompts_dir / f"observe_batch_{i}.md"
    77→        safe_write_text(prompt_file, prompt)
    78→
    79→        output_file = output_dir / f"observe_batch_{i}.raw.txt"
    80→        log_file = logs_dir / f"observe_batch_{i}.log"
    81→        batch_meta.append((dims, output_file))
    82→
    83→        if not dry_run:
    84→            tasks[i] = partial(
    85→                run_triage_stage,
    86→                prompt=prompt,
    87→                repo_root=repo_root,
    88→                output_file=output_file,
    89→                log_file=log_file,
    90→                timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
    91→                validate_output_fn=_output_file_has_text,
    92→            )
    93→
    94→        dim_list = ", ".join(dims)
    95→        print(colorize(f"    Batch {i + 1}: {len(issues_subset)} issues ({dim_list})", "dim"))
    96→        _log(f"observe-batch batch={i + 1} issues={len(issues_subset)} dims={dim_list}")
    97→
    98→    if dry_run:
    99→        print(colorize("  [dry-run] Would execute parallel observe batches.", "dim"))
   100→        return TriageStageRunResult(exit_code=0, reason="dry_run", dry_run=True)
   101→
   102→    def _heartbeat(event: BatchProgressEvent) -> None:
   103→        details = event.details or {}
   104→        active = details.get("active_batches", [])
   105→        elapsed_map = details.get("elapsed_seconds", {})
   106→        if active:
   107→            parts = [f"#{i + 1}:{int(elapsed_map.get(i, 0))}s" for i in active[:6]]
   108→            print(colorize(f"    Observe heartbeat: {len(active)}/{total} active ({', '.join(parts)})", "dim"))
   109→
   110→    def _batch_label(idx: int) -> str:
   111→        return f"batch {idx + 1}/{total}"
   112→
   113→    failures = run_parallel_batches(
   114→        tasks=tasks,
   115→        stage_label="Observe",
   116→        batch_label_fn=_batch_label,
   117→        append_run_log=_log,
   118→        heartbeat_seconds=15.0,
   119→        heartbeat_printer=_heartbeat,
   120→    )
   121→
   122→    if failures:
   123→        print(colorize(f"  Observe: {len(failures)} batch(es) failed: {failures}", "red"))
   124→        for idx in failures:
   125→            log_file = logs_dir / f"observe_batch_{idx}.log"
   126→            print(colorize(f"    Check log: {log_file}", "dim"))
   127→        _log(f"observe-parallel-failed failures={failures}")
   128→        return TriageStageRunResult(
   129→            exit_code=1,
   130→            reason="parallel_execution_failed",
   131→        )
   132→
   133→    merged = _merge_observe_outputs(batch_meta)
   134→    print(colorize(f"  Observe: merged {total} batch outputs ({len(merged)} chars).", "green"))
   135→    _log(f"observe-parallel-done merged_chars={len(merged)}")
   136→    return TriageStageRunResult(
   137→        exit_code=0,
   138→        merged_output=merged,
   139→    )
   140→
   141→
   142→__all__ = ["run_observe"]
   143→
```

```
1→"""Basic triage stage confirmation handlers (observe/reflect + attestation parsing)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     8→from desloppify.base.output.user_message import print_user_message
     9→
    10→from .shared import (
    11→    StageConfirmationRequest,
    12→    ensure_stage_is_confirmable,
    13→    finalize_stage_confirmation,
    14→)
    15→from ..observe_batches import observe_dimension_breakdown
    16→from ..services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    17→
    18→# Observe verdicts that trigger auto-skip on confirmation
    19→_AUTO_SKIP_VERDICTS = frozenset({"false positive", "exaggerated"})
    20→
    21→
    22→MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN = 80
    23→
    24→
    25→def _find_referenced_names(text: str, names: list[str] | None) -> list[str]:
    26→    if not names:
    27→        return []
    28→    return [
    29→        name for name in names
    30→        if name.lower().replace("_", " ") in text or name.lower() in text
    31→    ]
    32→
    33→
    34→def _validate_observe_attestation(text: str, dimensions: list[str] | None) -> str | None:
    35→    found = _find_referenced_names(text, dimensions)
    36→    if found or not dimensions:
    37→        return None
    38→    dim_list = ", ".join(dimensions[:6])
    39→    return f"Attestation must reference at least one dimension from the summary. Mention one of: {dim_list}"
    40→
    41→
    42→def _validate_reflect_attestation(
    43→    text: str,
    44→    *,
    45→    dimensions: list[str] | None,
    46→    cluster_names: list[str] | None,
    47→) -> str | None:
    48→    refs = _find_referenced_names(text, dimensions) + _find_referenced_names(text, cluster_names)
    49→    if refs or not (dimensions or cluster_names):
    50→        return None
    51→    return (
    52→        "Attestation must reference at least one dimension or cluster name.\n"
    53→        f"  Valid dimensions: {', '.join((dimensions or [])[:6])}\n"
    54→        f"  Valid clusters: {', '.join((cluster_names or [])[:6]) if cluster_names else '(none yet)'}"
    55→    )
    56→
    57→
    58→def _validate_cluster_attestation(
    59→    text: str,
    60→    *,
    61→    cluster_names: list[str] | None,
    62→    action: str,
    63→) -> str | None:
    64→    found = _find_referenced_names(text, cluster_names)
    65→    if found or not cluster_names:
    66→        return None
    67→    names = ", ".join(cluster_names[:6])
    68→    return f"Attestation must reference at least one cluster you {action}. Mention one of: {names}"
    69→
    70→
    71→def validate_attestation(
    72→    attestation: str,
    73→    stage: str,
    74→    *,
    75→    dimensions: list[str] | None = None,
    76→    cluster_names: list[str] | None = None,
    77→) -> str | None:
    78→    """Return error message if attestation doesn't reference required data."""
    79→    text = attestation.lower()
    80→    validators = {
    81→        "observe": lambda: _validate_observe_attestation(text, dimensions),
    82→        "reflect": lambda: _validate_reflect_attestation(
    83→            text,
```

```
1→"""Review-oriented shared detector phases (dupes, security, coverage, etc.)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import rel
     8→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import log
     9→from desloppify.engine.detectors.dupes import detect_duplicates
    10→from desloppify.engine.detectors.jscpd_adapter import detect_with_jscpd
    11→from desloppify.engine.detectors.security.detector import detect_security_issues
    12→from desloppify.engine.detectors.test_coverage.detector import detect_test_coverage
    13→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import make_issue
    14→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import EXCLUDED_ZONES, filter_entries
    15→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import LangRuntimeContract
    16→from desloppify.languages._framework.issue_factories import make_dupe_issues
    17→from desloppify.state_io import Issue
    18→
    19→from .shared_phases_helpers import (
    20→    _entries_to_issues,
    21→    _filter_boilerplate_entries_by_zone,
    22→    _find_external_test_files,
    23→    _log_phase_summary,
    24→    _record_detector_coverage,
    25→)
    26→
    27→
    28→def phase_dupes(path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
    29→    """Shared phase runner: detect duplicate functions via lang.extract_functions."""
    30→    functions = lang.extract_functions(path)
    31→
    32→    if lang.zone_map is not None:
    33→        before = len(functions)
    34→        functions = [
    35→            function
    36→            for function in functions
    37→            if lang.zone_map.get(getattr(function, "file", "")) not in EXCLUDED_ZONES
    38→        ]
    39→        excluded = before - len(functions)
    40→        if excluded:
    41→            log(f"         zones: {excluded} functions excluded (non-production)")
    42→
    43→    entries, total_functions = detect_duplicates(functions)
    44→    issues = make_dupe_issues(entries, log)
    45→    return issues, {"dupes": total_functions}
    46→
    47→
    48→def phase_boilerplate_duplication(
    49→    path: Path,
    50→    lang: LangRuntimeContract,
    51→) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
    52→    """Shared phase runner: detect repeated boilerplate code via jscpd."""
    53→    entries = detect_with_jscpd(path)
    54→    if entries is None:
    55→        return [], {}
    56→    entries = _filter_boilerplate_entries_by_zone(entries, lang.zone_map)
    57→
    58→    issues: list[Issue] = []
    59→    for entry in entries:
    60→        locations = entry["locations"]
    61→        first = locations[0]
    62→        loc_preview = ", ".join(
    63→            f"{rel(item['file'])}:{item['line']}" for item in locations[:4]
    64→        )
    65→        if len(locations) > 4:
    66→            loc_preview += f", +{len(locations) - 4} more"
    67→        issues.append(
    68→            make_issue(
    69→                "boilerplate_duplication",
    70→                first["file"],
    71→                entry["id"],
    72→                tier=3,
    73→                confidence="medium",
    74→                summary=(
    75→                    f"Boilerplate block repeated across {entry['distinct_files']} files "
    76→                    f"(window {entry['window_size']} lines): {loc_preview}"
    77→                ),
    78→                detail={
    79→                    "distinct_files": entry["distinct_files"],
    80→                    "window_size": entry["window_size"],
    81→                    "locations": locations,
    82→                    "sample": entry["sample"],
    83→                },
    84→            )
    85→        )
    86→
    87→    if issues:
    88→        log(f"         boilerplate duplication: {len(issues)} clusters")
    89→    distinct_files = len({loc["file"] for entry in entries for loc in entry["locations"]})
    90→    return issues, {"boilerplate_duplication": distinct_files}
    91→
    92→
    93→def phase_security(path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
    94→    """Shared phase: detect security issues (cross-language + lang-specific)."""
    95→    zone_map = lang.zone_map
    96→    files = lang.file_finder(path) if lang.file_finder else []
    97→    entries, cross_lang_scanned = detect_security_issues(
    98→        files,
    99→        zone_map,
   100→        lang.name,
   101→        scan_root=path,
   102→    )
   103→    lang_scanned = 0
   104→
   105→    lang_result = lang.detect_lang_security_detailed(files, zone_map)
   106→    lang_entries = lang_result.entries
   107→    lang_scanned = max(0, int(lang_result.files_scanned))
   108→    _record_detector_coverage(lang, lang_result.coverage)
   109→    entries.extend(lang_entries)
   110→
   111→    entries = filter_entries(zone_map, entries, "security")
   112→    potential = max(cross_lang_scanned, lang_scanned)
   113→
   114→    results = _entries_to_issues(
   115→        "security",
   116→        entries,
   117→        include_zone=True,
   118→        zone_map=zone_map,
   119→    )
   120→    _log_phase_summary("security", results, potential, "files scanned")
   121→
   122→    if "security" not in lang.detector_coverage:
   123→        lang.detector_coverage["security"] = {
   124→            "detector": "security",
   125→            "status": "full",
   126→            "confidence": 1.0,
   127→            "summary": "Security coverage complete for enabled detectors.",
   128→            "impact": "",
   129→            "remediation": "",
   130→            "tool": "",
```

```
1→"""Python complexity signal compute functions.
     2→
     3→Used by ComplexitySignal definitions in __init__.py for the structural phase.
     4→"""
     5→
     6→import re
     7→from math import gcd
     8→
     9→
    10→def compute_max_params(content: str, lines: list[str]) -> tuple[int, str] | None:
    11→    """Find the function with the most parameters. Returns (count, label) or None."""
    12→    def_re = re.compile(r"def\s+\w+\s*\(", re.MULTILINE)
    13→    max_params = 0
    14→    for m in def_re.finditer(content):
    15→        # Track paren depth to find matching close-paren (handles nested parens)
    16→        depth = 1
    17→        start = m.end()
    18→        i = start
    19→        while i < len(content) and depth > 0:
    20→            ch = content[i]
    21→            if ch == "(":
    22→                depth += 1
    23→            elif ch == ")":
    24→                depth -= 1
    25→            i += 1
    26→        if depth != 0:
    27→            continue
    28→        param_str = content[start : i - 1]
    29→        params = [p.strip() for p in param_str.split(",") if p.strip()]
    30→        real_params = [
    31→            p for p in params if p not in ("self", "cls") and not p.startswith("*")
    32→        ]
    33→        if len(real_params) > max_params:
    34→            max_params = len(real_params)
    35→    if max_params > 7:
    36→        return max_params, f"function with {max_params} params"
    37→    return None
    38→
    39→
    40→def _detect_indent_unit(lines: list[str]) -> int:
    41→    """Detect indentation unit from file content using GCD of indentations."""
    42→    indents = set()
    43→    for line in lines:
    44→        stripped = line.lstrip()
    45→        if stripped and not stripped.startswith("#"):
    46→            indent = len(line) - len(stripped)
    47→            if 0 < indent <= 16:
    48→                indents.add(indent)
    49→    if not indents:
    50→        return 4
    51→    unit = 0
    52→    for i in indents:
    53→        unit = gcd(unit, i)
    54→    return max(unit, 1)
    55→
    56→
    57→def compute_nesting_depth(content: str, lines: list[str]) -> tuple[int, str] | None:
    58→    """Find maximum nesting depth by indentation. Returns (depth, label) or None."""
    59→    indent_unit = _detect_indent_unit(lines)
    60→    max_depth = 0
    61→    for line in lines:
    62→        stripped = line.lstrip()
    63→        if not stripped or stripped.startswith("#"):
    64→            continue
    65→        indent = len(line) - len(stripped)
    66→        depth = indent // indent_unit
    67→        if depth > max_depth:
    68→            max_depth = depth
    69→    if max_depth > 4:
    70→        return max_depth, f"nesting depth {max_depth}"
    71→    return None
    72→
    73→
    74→def compute_long_functions(content: str, lines: list[str]) -> tuple[int, str] | None:
    75→    """Find functions >80 LOC. Returns (longest_loc, label) or None."""
    76→    results = []
    77→    fn_re = re.compile(r"^(\s*)def\s+(\w+)")
    78→
    79→    i = 0
    80→    while i < len(lines):
    81→        m = fn_re.match(lines[i])
    82→        if not m:
    83→            i += 1
    84→            continue
    85→
    86→        fn_indent = len(m.group(1))
    87→        fn_name = m.group(2)
    88→        fn_start = i
    89→
    90→        j = i + 1
    91→        while j < len(lines):
    92→            if lines[j].strip() == "":
    93→                j += 1
    94→                continue
    95→            line_indent = len(lines[j]) - len(lines[j].lstrip())
    96→            if line_indent <= fn_indent and lines[j].strip():
    97→                break
    98→            j += 1
    99→
   100→        fn_loc = j - fn_start
   101→        if fn_loc > 80:
   102→            results.append((fn_name, fn_loc))
   103→        i = j
   104→
   105→    if results:
   106→        longest = max(results, key=lambda x: x[1])
   107→        return longest[1], f"long function ({longest[0]}: {longest[1]} LOC)"
   108→    return None
   109→
```

```
1→"""Bandit adapter — Python security detection via the bandit static analyser.
     2→
     3→Runs ``bandit -r -f json --quiet <path>`` as a subprocess and converts its JSON
     4→output into the security entry dicts expected by ``phase_security``.
     5→
     6→Bandit covers AST-level security checks (shell injection, unsafe deserialization,
     7→SQL injection, etc.) more reliably than custom regex/AST patterns. When bandit is
     8→installed, it is used as the lang-specific security detector; otherwise
     9→Python-specific security checks will be skipped.
    10→
    11→Bandit severity → desloppify tier/confidence mapping:
    12→  HIGH   → tier=4, confidence="high"
    13→  MEDIUM → tier=3, confidence="medium"
    14→  LOW    → tier=3, confidence="low"
    15→
    16→The ``check_id`` in the entry detail is the bandit test ID (e.g., "B602") so
    17→issues are stable across reruns and can be wontfix-tracked by ID.
    18→"""
    19→
    20→from __future__ import annotations
    21→
    22→import json
    23→import logging
    24→import subprocess  # nosec B404
    25→import sys
    26→from dataclasses import dataclass
    27→from pathlib import Path
    28→from typing import Literal
    29→
    30→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import rel
    31→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    32→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import FileZoneMap, Zone
    33→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import DetectorCoverageStatus

    38→_SEVERITY_TO_CONFIDENCE = {"HIGH": "high", "MEDIUM": "medium", "LOW": "low"}
    39→
    40→# Bandit test IDs that overlap with the cross-language security detector
```

```
211→def build_triage_snapshot(plan: dict, state: StateModel) -> TriageSnapshot:
   212→    """Build a canonical triage snapshot from plan and state."""
   213→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   214→    triaged_ids = set(_normalized_issue_id_list(meta.get("triaged_ids")))
   215→    frozen_ids = _explicit_active_triage_issue_ids(plan) & open_review_ids(state)
   216→    live_open = open_review_ids(state)
   217→    known_ids = triaged_ids | frozen_ids
   218→    new_since = live_open - known_ids if known_ids else set()
   219→    in_scope_ids = coverage_open_ids(plan, state)
   220→    organized_count, total_in_scope, _clusters = triage_coverage(
   221→        plan,
   222→        open_review_ids=in_scope_ids,
   223→    )
   224→    has_triage_in_queue = bool(set(plan.get("queue_order", [])) & TRIAGE_IDS)
   225→    has_completed_artifact = bool(
   226→        meta.get("last_completed_at")
   227→        or meta.get("last_triage")
   228→        or meta.get("strategy_summary")
   229→    )
   230→    lifecycle_state = _resolve_triage_lifecycle_state(
   231→        live_open=live_open,
   232→        triaged_ids=triaged_ids,
   233→        has_triage_in_queue=has_triage_in_queue,
   234→        is_triage_stale=bool(new_since),
   235→        has_completed_artifact=has_completed_artifact,
   236→    )
   237→
   238→    return TriageSnapshot(
   239→        progress=compute_triage_progress(meta.get("triage_stages", {})),
   240→        frozen_issue_ids=frozenset(frozen_ids),
   241→        live_open_ids=frozenset(live_open),
   242→        new_since_triage_ids=frozenset(new_since),
   243→        undispositioned_ids=frozenset(undispositioned_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)),
   244→        organized_count=organized_count,
   245→        total_in_scope=total_in_scope,
   246→        has_triage_in_queue=has_triage_in_queue,
   247→        is_triage_stale=bool(new_since),
   248→        cycle_active=bool(frozen_ids),
   249→        triage_has_run=bool(triaged_ids),
   250→        lifecycle_state=lifecycle_state,
   251→        requires_plan_before_execution=(
   252→            bool(live_open) and lifecycle_state == TRIAGE_LIFECYCLE_PENDING
   253→        ),
   254→    )
   255→
   256→
   257→__all__ = [

   259→    "TRIAGE_LIFECYCLE_COMPLETED",
```

```
    33→def build_import_load_config(

    35→    lang_name: str | None,
    36→    import_config: ReviewImportConfig,

    43→        trusted_assessment_source=import_config.trusted_assessment_source,
    44→        trusted_assessment_label=import_config.trusted_assessment_label,
    45→        attested_external=import_config.attested_external,
    46→        manual_override=override_enabled,
    47→        manual_attest=override_attest,

    49→
    50→
    51→def validate_import_flag_combos(
    52→    *,
    53→    attested_external: bool,
    54→    allow_partial: bool,
    55→    override_enabled: bool,
    56→    override_attest: str | None,
    57→) -> None:
    58→    """Fail fast on conflicting import flags to keep behavior explicit."""
    59→    if attested_external and override_enabled:
    60→        raise ImportFlagValidationError(
    61→            "--attested-external cannot be combined with --manual-override"
    62→        )
    63→    if attested_external and allow_partial:
    64→        raise ImportFlagValidationError(
    65→            "--attested-external cannot be combined with --allow-partial"
    66→        )
    67→    if override_enabled and allow_partial:

    70→        )
    71→    if override_enabled and (

    73→    ):
    74→        raise ImportFlagValidationError("--manual-override requires --attest")

    76→
    77→def imported_assessment_keys(issues_data: NormalizedReviewImportPayload) -> set[str]:
    78→    """Return normalized assessment dimension keys from payload."""
    79→    raw_assessments = issues_data["assessments"]
    80→    keys: set[str] = set()
    81→    for raw_key in raw_assessments:
    82→        normalized = normalize_dimension_name(str(raw_key))
    83→        if normalized:
    84→            keys.add(normalized)
    85→    return keys
    86→
    87→
    88→def mark_manual_override_assessments_provisional(
    89→    state: dict,
    90→    *,
    91→    assessment_keys: set[str],
    92→) -> int:
    93→    """Mark imported manual override assessments as provisional until next scan."""
    94→    if not assessment_keys:
    95→        return 0
    96→    store = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    97→    if not isinstance(store, dict):
    98→        return 0
    99→
   100→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   101→    expires_scan = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0) + 1
   102→    marked = 0
   103→    for key in sorted(assessment_keys):
   104→        payload = store.get(key)
   105→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   106→            continue
   107→        payload["source"] = "manual_override"
   108→        payload["assessed_at"] = now
   109→        payload["provisional_override"] = True
   110→        payload["provisional_until_scan"] = expires_scan
   111→        payload.pop("placeholder", None)
   112→        marked += 1
   113→    return marked
   114→
   115→
   116→def clear_provisional_override_flags(
   117→    state: dict,
   118→    *,
   119→    assessment_keys: set[str],
   120→) -> int:
   121→    """Clear provisional override flags when trusted assessments replace them."""
   122→    if not assessment_keys:
   123→        return 0
   124→    store = state.get("subjective_assessments")
   125→    if not isinstance(store, dict):
   126→        return 0
   127→
   128→    cleared = 0
   129→    for key in sorted(assessment_keys):
   130→        payload = store.get(key)
   131→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   132→            continue
   133→        if payload.pop("provisional_override", None) is not None:
   134→            cleared += 1
   135→        payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   136→        if payload.get("source") == "manual_override":
   137→            payload["source"] = "holistic"
   138→    return cleared
   139→
   140→
```

```
1→"""Canonical confirmation-stage router for triage."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from .basic import (
     8→    MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN,
     9→    confirm_observe,
    10→    confirm_reflect,
    11→    validate_attestation,
    12→)
    13→from .enrich import confirm_enrich, confirm_sense_check
    14→from .organize import confirm_organize
    15→from ..services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    16→
    17→
    18→def cmd_confirm_stage(
    19→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    20→    *,
    21→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
    22→) -> None:
    23→    """Route ``--confirm <stage>`` to the stage-specific confirmation handler."""
    24→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
    25→    confirm_stage = getattr(args, "confirm", None)
    26→    attestation = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
    27→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
    28→    stages = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("triage_stages", {})
    29→
    30→    handlers = {
    31→        "observe": confirm_observe,
    32→        "reflect": confirm_reflect,
    33→        "organize": confirm_organize,
    34→        "enrich": confirm_enrich,
    35→        "sense-check": confirm_sense_check,
    36→    }
    37→    handler = handlers.get(confirm_stage)
    38→    if handler is None:
    39→        return
    40→    handler(args, plan, stages, attestation, services=resolved_services)
    41→
    42→
    43→__all__ = ["MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN", "cmd_confirm_stage", "validate_attestation"]
    44→
```

```
1→"""Plan persistence — load/save with atomic writes."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→import os
     8→import shutil
     9→import sys
    10→from collections.abc import Iterator
    11→from contextlib import contextmanager
    12→from dataclasses import dataclass
    13→from pathlib import Path
    14→from typing import cast
    15→
    16→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    17→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import safe_write_text
    18→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    19→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    20→    PLAN_VERSION,
    21→    PlanModel,
    22→    empty_plan,
    23→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    24→    validate_plan,
    25→)
    26→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    27→    get_state_dir,
    28→    json_default,
    29→    utc_now,
    30→)
    31→
    32→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    33→
    34→_PLAN_FILE_SENTINEL = object()
    35→PLAN_FILE = _PLAN_FILE_SENTINEL
    36→_INITIAL_PLAN_FILE = _PLAN_FILE_SENTINEL
    37→
    38→
    39→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    40→class PlanLoadStatus:
    41→    """Resolved plan load result with degraded-mode signaling."""
    42→
    43→    plan: PlanModel | None
    44→    degraded: bool
    45→    error_kind: str | None = None
    46→
    47→
    48→def get_plan_file() -> Path:
    49→    """Return the default plan file for the current runtime context."""
    50→    return get_state_dir() / "plan.json"
    51→
    52→
    53→def _default_plan_file() -> Path:
    54→    """Resolve the effective default plan path.
    55→
    56→    If tests monkeypatch ``PLAN_FILE`` in this module, use the patched value.
    57→    """
    58→    if PLAN_FILE != _INITIAL_PLAN_FILE:
    59→        return Path(PLAN_FILE)
    60→    return get_plan_file()
    61→
    62→
    63→@contextmanager
    64→def plan_lock(path: Path | None = None) -> Iterator[None]:
    65→    """Acquire exclusive lock on plan file for read-modify-write safety."""
    66→    plan_path = path or _default_plan_file()
    67→    lock_path = plan_path.with_suffix(".lock")
    68→    lock_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    69→    fd = os.open(str(lock_path), os.O_CREAT | os.O_WRONLY)
    70→    try:
    71→        if sys.platform == "win32":
    72→            import msvcrt
    73→
    74→            msvcrt.locking(fd, msvcrt.LK_LOCK, 1)
    75→        else:
    76→            import fcntl
    77→
    78→            fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
    79→        yield
    80→    finally:
    81→        if sys.platform == "win32":
    82→            import msvcrt
    83→
    84→            msvcrt.locking(fd, msvcrt.LK_UNLCK, 1)
    85→        else:
    86→            import fcntl
    87→
    88→            fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_UN)
    89→        os.close(fd)
    90→
    91→
    92→def load_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> PlanModel:
    93→    """Load plan from disk, or return empty plan on missing/corruption."""
    94→    plan_path = path or _default_plan_file()
    95→    if not plan_path.exists():
    96→        return empty_plan()
    97→
    98→    try:
    99→        data = json.loads(plan_path.read_text())
   100→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError) as ex:
   101→        # Try backup before giving up
   102→        backup = plan_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
   103→        if backup.exists():
   104→            try:
   105→                data = json.loads(backup.read_text())
   106→                logger.warning("Plan file corrupted (%s), loaded from backup.", ex)
   107→                print(f"  Warning: Plan file corrupted ({ex}), loaded from backup.", file=sys.stderr)
   108→                # Fall through to validation below
   109→            except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError) as backup_ex:
   110→                logger.warning("Plan file and backup both corrupted: %s / %s", ex, backup_ex)
   111→                print(f"  Warning: Plan file corrupted ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
   112→                return empty_plan()
   113→        else:
   114→            logger.warning("Plan file corrupted (%s). Starting fresh.", ex)
   115→            print(f"  Warning: Plan file corrupted ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
   116→            return empty_plan()
   117→
   118→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
   119→        logger.warning("Plan file root is not a JSON object. Starting fresh.")
   120→        print("  Warning: Plan file root must be a JSON object. Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
   121→        return empty_plan()
   122→
   123→    version = data.get("version", 1)
   124→    if version > PLAN_VERSION:
   125→        logger.warning("Plan file version %d > supported %d.", version, PLAN_VERSION)
   126→        print(
   127→            f"  Warning: Plan file version {version} is newer than supported "
   128→            f"({PLAN_VERSION}). Some features may not work correctly.",
   129→            file=sys.stderr,
   130→        )
   131→
   132→    ensure_plan_defaults(data)
   133→    try:
   134→        validate_plan(data)
   135→    except ValueError as ex:
   136→        logger.warning("Plan invariants invalid (%s). Starting fresh.", ex)
   137→        print(f"  Warning: Plan invariants invalid ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
   138→        return empty_plan()
   139→
   140→    return cast(PlanModel, data)
   141→
   142→
   143→def resolve_plan_load_status(path: Path | None = None) -> PlanLoadStatus:
   144→    """Load a plan with explicit degraded-mode metadata."""
   145→    plan_path = path or _default_plan_file()
   146→    if not plan_path.exists():
   147→        return PlanLoadStatus(plan=None, degraded=False, error_kind=None)
   148→    try:
   149→        return PlanLoadStatus(
   150→            plan=load_plan(plan_path),
   151→            degraded=False,
   152→            error_kind=None,
   153→        )
   154→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   155→        return PlanLoadStatus(
   156→            plan=None,
   157→            degraded=True,
   158→            error_kind=exc.__class__.__name__,
   159→        )
   160→
   161→
   162→def save_plan(plan: PlanModel | dict, path: Path | None = None) -> None:
   163→    """Validate and save plan to disk atomically."""
   164→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   165→    plan["updated"] = utc_now()
   166→    validate_plan(plan)
   167→
   168→    plan_path = path or _default_plan_file()
   169→    plan_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   170→
   171→    content = json.dumps(plan, indent=2, default=json_default) + "\n"
   172→
   173→    if plan_path.exists():
   174→        backup = plan_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
   175→        try:
   176→            shutil.copy2(str(plan_path), str(backup))
   177→        except OSError as backup_ex:
   178→            log_best_effort_failure(logger, "create plan backup", backup_ex)
   179→
   180→    try:
   181→        safe_write_text(plan_path, content)
   182→    except OSError as ex:
   183→        print(f"  Warning: Could not save plan: {ex}", file=sys.stderr)
   184→        raise
   185→
   186→
   187→def plan_path_for_state(state_path: Path) -> Path:
   188→    """Derive plan.json path from a state file path."""
   189→    return state_path.parent / "plan.json"
   190→
   191→
   192→def has_living_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> bool:
   193→    """Return True if a plan.json exists and has user intent."""
   194→    plan_path = path or _default_plan_file()
   195→    if not plan_path.exists():
   196→        return False
   197→    plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   198→    return bool(
   199→        plan.get("queue_order")
   200→        or plan.get("overrides")
   201→        or plan.get("clusters")
   202→    )
   203→
   204→
   205→__all__ = [
   206→    "PLAN_FILE",
   207→    "PlanLoadStatus",
   208→    "get_plan_file",
   209→    "has_living_plan",
   210→    "load_plan",
   211→    "plan_lock",
   212→    "plan_path_for_state",
   213→    "resolve_plan_load_status",
   214→    "save_plan",
   215→]
   216→
```

```
     2→
     3→This package owns plan internals: schema, operations, triage internals, and
     4→sync policy. Callers should route through focused public facades where they
     5→exist (for example ``engine.plan_state``, ``engine.plan_ops``,

    10→- operations: queue/skip/cluster/meta/lifecycle mutations
    11→- triage: whole-plan triage implementation + staged triage playbook

    13→- sync: queue sync modules (context, dimensions, triage, workflow)

    15→Other modules:
```

```
    25→    args: argparse.Namespace,
```

```
    16→def _clean_judgment(raw: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    17→    """Validate and clean a dimension judgment payload. Returns None if empty."""
    18→    strengths_raw = raw.get("strengths")
    19→    strengths: list[str] = []
    20→    if isinstance(strengths_raw, list):
    21→        strengths = [
    22→            str(s).strip()
    23→            for s in strengths_raw[:5]
    24→            if isinstance(s, str) and str(s).strip()
    25→        ]
    26→
    27→    # Accept dimension_character (new) or issue_character (legacy)
    28→    dimension_character = ""
    29→    dc = raw.get("dimension_character")
    30→    if isinstance(dc, str) and dc.strip():
    31→        dimension_character = dc.strip()
    32→
    33→    issue_character = ""
    34→    ic = raw.get("issue_character")
    35→    if isinstance(ic, str) and ic.strip():
    36→        issue_character = ic.strip()
    37→
    38→    score_rationale = ""
    39→    sr = raw.get("score_rationale")
    40→    if isinstance(sr, str) and sr.strip():
    41→        score_rationale = sr.strip()
    42→
    43→    if not strengths and not dimension_character and not issue_character and not score_rationale:
    44→        return None
    45→
    46→    result: dict[str, Any] = {}
    47→    if strengths:
    48→        result["strengths"] = strengths
    49→    # Store dimension_character, falling back to issue_character
    50→    effective_dim_char = dimension_character or issue_character
    51→    if effective_dim_char:
    52→        result["dimension_character"] = effective_dim_char
    53→    if issue_character and not dimension_character:
    54→        result["issue_character"] = issue_character
    55→    if score_rationale:
    56→        result["score_rationale"] = score_rationale
    57→    return result

    60→def store_assessments(
    61→    state: StateModel,
    62→    assessments: dict[str, Any],

    64→    *,
    65→    utc_now_fn=utc_now,
    66→    dimension_judgment: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] | None = None,

    70→    *assessments*: ``{dim_name: score}`` or ``{dim_name: {score, ...}}``.

    73→
    74→    Holistic assessments overwrite per-file for the same dimension.

   100→            ]

   103→        cleaned_scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   104→        if isinstance(component_scores, dict):
   105→            for key, raw in component_scores.items():

   128→def store_context_updates(
   129→    state: StateModel,
   130→    context_updates: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] | None,
   131→    *,
   132→    valid_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
   133→    utc_now_fn=utc_now,
   134→    source: str = "holistic",
   135→) -> None:
   136→    """Apply per-dimension context updates (add/remove/settle/unsettle) to state."""
   137→    if not context_updates:
   138→        return
   139→
   140→    all_contexts: dict[str, DimensionContext] = state.setdefault("dimension_contexts", {})
   141→    now = utc_now_fn()
   142→
   143→    for dim_name, updates in context_updates.items():
   144→        if not isinstance(updates, dict):
   145→            continue
   146→        [REDACTED](str(dim_name))
   147→        if not dim_key:
   148→            continue
   149→        if valid_dimensions is not None and dim_key not in valid_dimensions:
   150→            continue
   151→
   152→        ctx: DimensionContext = all_contexts.get(dim_key, {})
   153→        is_new = dim_key not in all_contexts
   154→        insights: list[ContextInsight] = list(ctx.get("insights", []))
   155→        changed = False
   156→
   157→        # Build header lookup (case-insensitive)
   158→        header_index = {
   159→            str(ins.get("header", "")).strip().lower(): i
   160→            for i, ins in enumerate(insights)
   161→        }
   162→
   163→        # Remove
   164→        for header in updates.get("remove", []):
   165→            if not isinstance(header, str):
   166→                continue
   167→            key = header.strip().lower()
   168→            if key in header_index:
   169→                idx = header_index[key]
   170→                insights.pop(idx)
   171→                # Rebuild index after removal
   172→                header_index = {
   173→                    str(ins.get("header", "")).strip().lower(): i
   174→                    for i, ins in enumerate(insights)
   175→                }
   176→                changed = True
   177→
   178→        # Add (dedup by header)
   179→        for item in updates.get("add", []):
   180→            if not isinstance(item, dict):
   181→                continue
   182→            header = str(item.get("header", "")).strip()
   183→            if not header:
   184→                continue
   185→            key = header.lower()
   186→            if key in header_index:
   187→                # Update existing insight's description
   188→                idx = header_index[key]
   189→                desc = str(item.get("description", "")).strip()
   190→                if desc and desc != str(insights[idx].get("description", "")).strip():
   191→                    insights[idx]["description"] = desc
   192→                    changed = True
   193→                if item.get("settled") is not None:
   194→                    insights[idx]["settled"] = bool(item["settled"])
   195→                    changed = True
   196→                if item.get("positive") is not None:
   197→                    insights[idx]["positive"] = bool(item["positive"])
   198→                    changed = True
   199→                continue
   200→            new_insight: ContextInsight = {
   201→                "header": header,
   202→                "description": str(item.get("description", "")).strip(),
   203→                "settled": bool(item.get("settled", False)),
   204→                "positive": bool(item.get("positive", False)),
   205→                "added_at": now,
   206→                "source": source,
   207→            }
   208→            insights.append(new_insight)
   209→            header_index[key] = len(insights) - 1
   210→            changed = True
   211→
   212→        # Settle
   213→        for header in updates.get("settle", []):
   214→            if not isinstance(header, str):
   215→                continue
   216→            key = header.strip().lower()
   217→            if key in header_index:
   218→                idx = header_index[key]
   219→                if not insights[idx].get("settled"):
   220→                    insights[idx]["settled"] = True
   221→                    changed = True
   222→
   223→        # Unsettle
   224→        for header in updates.get("unsettle", []):
   225→            if not isinstance(header, str):
   226→                continue
   227→            key = header.strip().lower()
   228→            if key in header_index:
   229→                idx = header_index[key]
   230→                if insights[idx].get("settled"):
   231→                    insights[idx]["settled"] = False
   232→                    changed = True
   233→
   234→        ctx["insights"] = insights
   235→        if changed:
   236→            ctx["stable_rounds"] = 0
   237→            ctx["updated_at"] = now
   238→        else:
   239→            ctx["stable_rounds"] = ctx.get("stable_rounds", 0) + 1
   240→
   241→        if is_new:
   242→            ctx["created_at"] = now
   243→            if "updated_at" not in ctx:
   244→                ctx["updated_at"] = now
   245→
   246→        all_contexts[dim_key] = ctx
   247→
   248→

   250→    state: StateModel,
   251→    context_updates: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
   252→) -> None:
   253→    """Derive judgment.strengths from positive context insights."""
   254→    assessments_store = state.get("subjective_assessments", {})
   255→    contexts_store = state.get("dimension_contexts", {})
   256→    for dim_name in context_updates:
   257→        [REDACTED](str(dim_name))
```
