```
    16→
    17→
    18→@dataclass
    19→class TriageInput:
    20→    """All data needed to produce/update triage clusters."""
    21→
    22→    open_issues: dict[str, dict]       # id -> issue (review + concerns)
    23→    mechanical_issues: dict[str, dict]  # id -> issue (non-review, for context)
    24→    existing_clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
    25→    dimension_scores: dict[str, Any]      # for context
    26→    new_since_last: set[str]             # issue IDs new since last triage
    27→    resolved_since_last: set[str]        # issue IDs resolved since last
    28→    previously_dismissed: list[str]      # IDs dismissed in prior triage
    29→    triage_version: int                  # next version number
    30→    resolved_issues: dict[str, dict]   # full issue objects for resolved IDs
    31→    completed_clusters: list[dict]       # clusters completed since last triage
    32→
    33→@dataclass
    34→class DismissedIssue:
    35→    """A issue the LLM says doesn't make sense."""
    36→
    37→    issue_id: str
    38→    reason: str
    39→
    40→@dataclass
    41→class ContradictionNote:
    42→    """Record of a resolved contradiction."""
    43→
    44→    kept: str
    45→    dismissed: str
    46→    reason: str
    47→
    48→@dataclass
    49→class TriageResult:
    50→    """Parsed and validated LLM triage output."""
    51→
    52→    strategy_summary: str
    53→    epics: list[dict]
    54→    dismissed_issues: list[DismissedIssue] = field(default_factory=list)
    55→    contradiction_notes: list[ContradictionNote] = field(default_factory=list)
    56→    priority_rationale: str = ""
    57→
    58→    @property
    59→    def clusters(self) -> list[dict]:
    60→        """Canonical cluster view for triage results."""
    61→        return self.epics
    62→
    63→
    64→def _issue_dimension(issue: dict) -> str:
    65→    detail = issue.get("detail", {})
    66→    if isinstance(detail, dict):
    67→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
    68→        if isinstance(dimension, str):
    69→            return dimension
    70→    return ""
    71→
    72→
    73→def _recurring_dimensions(
    74→    open_issues: dict[str, dict],
    75→    resolved_issues: dict[str, dict],
    76→) -> dict[str, dict[str, list[str]]]:
    77→    open_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    78→    for issue_id, issue in open_issues.items():
    79→        dimension = _issue_dimension(issue)
    80→        if dimension:
    81→            open_by_dim.setdefault(dimension, []).append(issue_id)
    82→
    83→    resolved_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    84→    for issue_id, issue in resolved_issues.items():
    85→        dimension = _issue_dimension(issue)
    86→        if dimension:
    87→            resolved_by_dim.setdefault(dimension, []).append(issue_id)
    88→
    89→    recurring: dict[str, dict[str, list[str]]] = {}
    90→    for dimension in sorted(set(open_by_dim) & set(resolved_by_dim)):
    91→        recurring[dimension] = {
    92→            "open": sorted(open_by_dim[dimension]),
    93→            "resolved": sorted(resolved_by_dim[dimension]),
    94→        }
    95→    return recurring
    96→
    97→
    98→def _split_open_issue_buckets(
    99→    issues: dict[str, dict],
   100→) -> tuple[dict[str, dict], dict[str, dict]]:
   101→    open_review: dict[str, dict] = {}
   102→    open_mechanical: dict[str, dict] = {}
   103→    for issue_id, issue in issues.items():
   104→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
   105→            continue
   106→        if issue.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns"):
   107→            open_review[issue_id] = issue
   108→            continue
   109→        open_mechanical[issue_id] = issue
   110→    return open_review, open_mechanical
   111→
   112→
   113→def _recent_completed_clusters(meta: dict, plan: PlanModel) -> list[dict]:
   114→    last_completed = meta.get("last_completed_at", "")
   115→    all_completed: list[dict] = plan.get("completed_clusters", [])
   116→    if not last_completed:
   117→        return list(all_completed)
   118→    return [
   119→        cluster for cluster in all_completed
   120→        if cluster.get("completed_at", "") > last_completed
   121→    ]
   122→
   123→
   124→def collect_triage_input(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> TriageInput:
   125→    """Gather all data needed for the triage LLM prompt."""
   126→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   127→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   128→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   129→    epics = triage_clusters(plan)
   130→    open_review, open_mechanical = _split_open_issue_buckets(issues)
   131→    snapshot = build_triage_snapshot(plan, state)
   132→
   133→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   134→    current_review_ids = set(open_review.keys())
   135→    new_since = set(snapshot.new_since_triage_ids)
   136→    resolved_since = triaged_ids - current_review_ids
   137→    previously_dismissed = list(meta.get("dismissed_ids", []))
   138→    version = int(meta.get("version", 0)) + 1
   139→
   140→    # Resolved issue objects (for REFLECT stage)
   141→    resolved_issue_objs = {
   142→        fid: issues[fid] for fid in resolved_since if fid in issues
   143→    }
   144→
   145→    return TriageInput(
   146→        open_issues=open_review,
   147→        mechanical_issues=open_mechanical,
   148→        existing_clusters=dict(epics),
   149→        dimension_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   150→        new_since_last=new_since,
   151→        resolved_since_last=resolved_since,
   152→        previously_dismissed=previously_dismissed,
   153→        triage_version=version,
   154→        resolved_issues=resolved_issue_objs,
   155→        completed_clusters=_recent_completed_clusters(meta, plan),
   156→    )
   157→
   158→_TRIAGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT = """\
   159→You are maintaining the meta-plan for this codebase. Produce a coherent
   160→prioritized strategy for all open review issues.
   161→
   162→Your plan should:
   163→- Cluster issues by ROOT CAUSE, not by dimension or detector
   164→- Give each cluster a clear thesis: one imperative sentence
   165→- Order clusters by dependency: what must be done first for later work to make sense
   166→- Dismiss issues that don't make sense, are contradictory, or are false positives
   167→- Mark which clusters are agent-safe (can be executed mechanically) vs need human judgment
   168→- Avoid creating work that contradicts other work in the plan
   169→- Be ambitious but realistic — aim to resolve all issues coherently
   170→
   171→Available directions for clusters: delete, merge, flatten, enforce, simplify, decompose, extract, inline.
   172→
   173→Available plan tools (the agent executing your plan has access to these):
   174→- `desloppify plan queue` — view the execution queue in priority order
   175→- `desloppify backlog` — inspect broader open work outside the execution queue
   176→- `desloppify plan focus <name>` — focus the queue on one cluster
   177→- `desloppify plan skip <id> --permanent --note "why" --attest "..."` — permanently dismiss
   178→- `desloppify plan skip <id> --note "revisit later"` — temporarily defer
   179→- `desloppify plan resolve <id> --note "what I did" --attest "..."` — mark resolved
   180→- `desloppify plan reorder <id> top|bottom|before|after <target>` — reorder
   181→- `desloppify plan cluster show <name>` — inspect a cluster
   182→- `desloppify scan` — re-scan after making changes to verify progress
   183→- `desloppify show review --status open` — see all open review issues
   184→
   185→Your output defines the ENTIRE work plan. Issues not assigned to any cluster
   186→will remain in the queue as individual items. Dismissed issues will be
   187→removed from the queue with your stated reason.
   188→
   189→Respond with a single JSON object matching this schema:
   190→{
   191→  "strategy_summary": "2-4 sentence narrative: what the meta-plan says, top priorities, current state",
   192→  "clusters": [
   193→    {
   194→      "name": "slug-name",
   195→      "thesis": "imperative one-liner",
   196→      "direction": "delete|merge|flatten|enforce|simplify|decompose|extract|inline",
   197→      "root_cause": "why this cluster exists",
   198→      "issue_ids": ["id1", "id2"],
   199→      "dismissed": ["id3"],
   200→      "agent_safe": true,
   201→      "dependency_order": 1,
   202→      "action_steps": [
   203→        {
   204→          "title": "Short imperative step title",
   205→          "detail": "Concrete implementation detail with file paths/locations",
   206→          "issue_refs": ["id1"]
   207→        }
   208→      ],
   209→      "status": "pending"
   210→    }
   211→  ],
   212→  "dismissed_issues": [
   213→    {"issue_id": "id", "reason": "why this issue doesn't make sense"}
   214→  ],
   215→  "contradiction_notes": [
   216→    {"kept": "issue_id", "dismissed": "issue_id", "reason": "why"}
   217→  ],
   218→  "priority_rationale": "why the dependency_order is what it is"
   219→}
   220→"""
   221→
   222→def _append_existing_clusters_section(parts: list[str], clusters: dict[str, Cluster]) -> None:
   223→    if not clusters:
   224→        return
   225→    parts.append("## Existing clusters")
   226→    for name, cluster in sorted(clusters.items()):
   227→        status = cluster.get("status", "pending")
   228→        thesis = cluster.get("thesis", "")
   229→        direction = cluster.get("direction", "")
   230→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   231→        parts.append(
   232→            f"- {name} [{status}] ({direction}): {thesis}"
   233→            f"\n  Issues: {', '.join(issue_ids[:10])}"
   234→            f"{'...' if len(issue_ids) > 10 else ''}"
   235→        )
   236→    parts.append("")
   237→
   238→
   239→def _append_changed_issue_section(
   240→    parts: list[str],
   241→    *,
   242→    title: str,
   243→    issue_ids: set[str],
   244→    issues: dict[str, dict] | None = None,
   245→) -> None:
   246→    if not issue_ids:
   247→        return
   248→    parts.append(f"## {title} ({len(issue_ids)})")
   249→    for issue_id in sorted(issue_ids):
   250→        if issues is None:
   251→            parts.append(f"- {issue_id}")
   252→            continue
   253→        issue = issues.get(issue_id, {})
   254→        parts.append(f"- {issue_id}: {issue.get('summary', '(no summary)')}")
   255→    parts.append("")
   256→
   257→
   258→def _append_resolved_issue_context(parts: list[str], resolved_issues: dict[str, dict]) -> None:
   259→    if not resolved_issues:
   260→        return
   261→    parts.append(
   262→        "## Resolved review issues available for recurrence context "
   263→        f"({len(resolved_issues)})"
   264→    )
   265→    resolved_ids = sorted(resolved_issues)
   266→    for issue_id in resolved_ids[:30]:
   267→        issue = resolved_issues.get(issue_id, {})
   268→        summary = str(issue.get("summary", "(no summary)"))
   269→        dimension = _issue_dimension(issue)
   270→        dim_suffix = f" [{dimension}]" if dimension else ""
   271→        parts.append(f"- {issue_id}{dim_suffix}: {summary}")
   272→    if len(resolved_ids) > 30:
   273→        parts.append(f"- ... and {len(resolved_ids) - 30} more resolved issues")
   274→    parts.append("")
   275→
   276→
   277→def _append_completed_clusters_section(parts: list[str], completed_clusters: list[dict]) -> None:
   278→    if not completed_clusters:
   279→        return
   280→    parts.append(
   281→        "## Completed clusters since last triage "
   282→        f"({len(completed_clusters)})"
   283→    )
   284→    completed = sorted(
   285→        completed_clusters,
   286→        key=lambda cluster: str(cluster.get("completed_at", "")),
   287→        reverse=True,
   288→    )
   289→    for cluster in completed[:10]:
   290→        name = str(cluster.get("name", "(unnamed cluster)"))
   291→        thesis = str(cluster.get("thesis") or cluster.get("description") or "")
   292→        completed_at = str(cluster.get("completed_at", ""))
   293→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   294→        issue_count = len(issue_ids) if isinstance(issue_ids, list) else 0
   295→        parts.append(
   296→            f"- {name}: {thesis} (issues: {issue_count}, completed_at: {completed_at})"
   297→        )
   298→    if len(completed) > 10:
   299→        parts.append(f"- ... and {len(completed) - 10} more completed clusters")
   300→    parts.append("")
   301→
   302→
   303→def _append_recurring_dimensions_section(
   304→    parts: list[str],
   305→    open_issues: dict[str, dict],
   306→    resolved_issues: dict[str, dict],
   307→) -> None:
   308→    recurring = _recurring_dimensions(open_issues, resolved_issues)
   309→    if not recurring:
   310→        return
   311→    parts.append(
   312→        "## Potential recurring dimensions (resolved issues still have open peers)"
   313→    )
   314→    for dimension, bucket in recurring.items():
   315→        open_count = len(bucket["open"])
   316→        resolved_count = len(bucket["resolved"])
   317→        parts.append(
   318→            f"- {dimension}: {open_count} open / {resolved_count} recently resolved"
   319→        )
   320→    parts.append("")
   321→
   322→
   323→def _append_open_review_issues_section(parts: list[str], open_issues: dict[str, dict]) -> None:
   324→    parts.append(f"## All open review issues ({len(open_issues)})")
   325→    for issue_id, issue in sorted(open_issues.items()):
   326→        detail = issue.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(issue.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   327→        suggestion = detail.get("suggestion", "")
   328→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
   329→        confidence = issue.get("confidence", "medium")
   330→        file_path = issue.get("file", "")
   331→        summary = issue.get("summary", "")
   332→        parts.append(f"- [{confidence}] {issue_id}")
   333→        parts.append(f"  File: {file_path}")
   334→        if dimension:
   335→            parts.append(f"  Dimension: {dimension}")
   336→        parts.append(f"  Summary: {summary}")
   337→        if suggestion:
   338→            parts.append(f"  Suggestion: {suggestion}")
   339→    parts.append("")
   340→
   341→
   342→def _append_dimension_scores_section(parts: list[str], dimension_scores: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   343→    if not dimension_scores:
   344→        return
   345→    parts.append("## Dimension scores (context)")
   346→    for name, data in sorted(dimension_scores.items()):
   347→        if not isinstance(data, dict):
   348→            continue
   349→        score = data.get("score", "?")
   350→        strict = data.get("strict", score)
   351→        issues = data.get("failing", 0)
   352→        parts.append(f"- {name}: {score}% (strict: {strict}%, {issues} issues)")
   353→    parts.append("")
   354→
   355→
   356→def _append_previously_dismissed_section(parts: list[str], dismissed_ids: list[str]) -> None:
   357→    if not dismissed_ids:
   358→        return
   359→    parts.append(f"## Previously dismissed ({len(dismissed_ids)})")
   360→    parts.append("Maintain unless contradicted by new evidence.")
   361→    for issue_id in dismissed_ids:
   362→        parts.append(f"- {issue_id}")
   363→    parts.append("")
   364→
   365→
   366→def build_triage_prompt(si: TriageInput) -> str:
   367→    """Build the user-facing prompt content with all issue data."""
   368→    parts: list[str] = []
   369→    _append_existing_clusters_section(parts, si.existing_clusters)
   370→    _append_changed_issue_section(
   371→        parts,
   372→        title="New issues since last triage",
   373→        issue_ids=si.new_since_last,
   374→        issues=si.open_issues,
   375→    )
   376→    _append_changed_issue_section(
   377→        parts,
   378→        title="Resolved since last triage",
   379→        issue_ids=si.resolved_since_last,
   380→    )
   381→    _append_resolved_issue_context(parts, si.resolved_issues)
   382→    _append_completed_clusters_section(parts, si.completed_clusters)
   383→    _append_recurring_dimensions_section(parts, si.open_issues, si.resolved_issues)
   384→    _append_open_review_issues_section(parts, si.open_issues)
   385→    _append_dimension_scores_section(parts, si.dimension_scores)
   386→    _append_previously_dismissed_section(parts, si.previously_dismissed)
   387→    return "\n".join(parts)
   388→
   389→__all__ = [
   390→    "_TRIAGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT",
   391→    "ContradictionNote",
   392→    "DismissedIssue",
   393→    "TriageInput",
   394→    "TriageResult",
   395→    "build_triage_prompt",
   396→    "collect_triage_input",
   397→]
   398→
```

```
1→"""Shared helpers for triage runner orchestrators."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from datetime import UTC, datetime
     6→
     7→STAGES: tuple[str, ...] = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check")
     8→
     9→
    10→def parse_only_stages(raw: str | None) -> list[str]:
    11→    """Parse --only-stages comma-separated string into validated stage list."""
    12→    if not raw:
    13→        return list(STAGES)
    14→    stages = [s.strip().lower() for s in raw.split(",") if s.strip()]
    15→    for stage in stages:
    16→        if stage not in STAGES:
    17→            raise ValueError(f"Unknown stage: {stage!r}. Valid: {', '.join(STAGES)}")
    18→    return stages
    19→
    20→
    21→def run_stamp() -> str:
    22→    return datetime.now(UTC).strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    23→
```

```
1→"""Helper utilities shared by detector phase runners."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import os
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→from typing import Any, Callable
     8→
     9→from desloppify.base.coercions import coerce_confidence
    10→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    11→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import log
    12→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import make_issue
    13→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import should_skip_issue
    14→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import DetectorCoverageStatus, LangRuntimeContract
    15→from desloppify.state_io import Issue
    16→
    17→
    18→def _filter_boilerplate_entries_by_zone(
    19→    entries: list[dict[str, Any]],
    20→    zone_map,
    21→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    22→    """Keep only in-scope, zone-allowed boilerplate clusters."""
    23→    if zone_map is None:
    24→        return entries
    25→
    26→    known_files = set(zone_map.all_files())
    27→    filtered: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    28→    skipped = 0
    29→    for entry in entries:
    30→        locations = entry.get("locations", [])
    31→        kept_locations = [
    32→            loc
    33→            for loc in locations
    34→            if loc.get("file") in known_files
    35→            and not should_skip_issue(
    36→                zone_map,
    37→                loc.get("file", ""),
    38→                "boilerplate_duplication",
    39→            )
    40→        ]
    41→        distinct_files = {loc["file"] for loc in kept_locations}
    42→        if len(distinct_files) < 2:
    43→            skipped += 1
    44→            continue
    45→
    46→        normalized = dict(entry)
    47→        normalized["locations"] = sorted(
    48→            kept_locations,
    49→            key=lambda item: (item.get("file", ""), item.get("line", 0)),
    50→        )
    51→        normalized["distinct_files"] = len(distinct_files)
    52→        filtered.append(normalized)
    53→
    54→    if skipped:
    55→        log(f"         zones: {skipped} boilerplate clusters excluded")
    56→    return filtered
    57→
    58→
    59→def _find_external_test_files(
    60→    path: Path,
    61→    lang: LangRuntimeContract,
    62→    *,
    63→    get_project_root_fn: Callable[[], Path] = get_project_root,
    64→) -> set[str]:
    65→    """Find test files in standard locations outside the scanned path."""
    66→    extra = set()
    67→    path_root = path.resolve()
    68→    project_root = get_project_root_fn()
    69→    test_dirs = lang.external_test_dirs or ["tests", "test"]
    70→    exts = tuple(lang.test_file_extensions or lang.extensions)
    71→    for test_dir in test_dirs:
    72→        directory = project_root / test_dir
    73→        if not directory.is_dir():
    74→            continue
    75→        if directory.resolve().is_relative_to(path_root):
    76→            continue
    77→        for root, _, files in os.walk(directory):
    78→            for filename in files:
    79→                if any(filename.endswith(ext) for ext in exts):
    80→                    extra.add(os.path.join(root, filename))
```

```
1→"""State mutation helpers for triage stage records."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

     5→from desloppify.state_io import utc_now
     6→
     7→from ..stage_queue import cascade_clear_later_confirmations
     8→
     9→
    10→def resolve_reusable_report(
    11→    report: str | None,
    12→    existing_stage: dict | None,
    13→) -> tuple[str | None, bool]:
    14→    if report:
    15→        return report, False
    16→    if existing_stage and existing_stage.get("report"):
    17→        return existing_stage["report"], True
    18→    return None, False
    19→
    20→
    21→def record_observe_stage(
    22→    stages: dict,
    23→    *,
    24→    report: str,
    25→    issue_count: int,
    26→    cited_ids: list[str],
    27→    existing_stage: dict | None,
    28→    is_reuse: bool,
    29→    assessments: list[dict] | None = None,
    30→) -> list[str]:
    31→    stages["observe"] = {
    32→        "stage": "observe",
    33→        "report": report,
    34→        "cited_ids": cited_ids,
    35→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
    36→        "issue_count": issue_count,
    37→    }
    38→    if assessments is not None:
    39→        stages["observe"]["assessments"] = assessments
    40→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
    41→        stages["observe"]["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
    42→        stages["observe"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
    43→    cleared = cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "observe")
    44→    if not is_reuse:
    45→        stages["observe"].pop("confirmed_at", None)
    46→        stages["observe"].pop("confirmed_text", None)
    47→    return cleared
    48→
    49→
    50→def record_organize_stage(
    51→    stages: dict,
    52→    *,
    53→    report: str,
    54→    issue_count: int,

    58→    stages["organize"] = {
    59→        "stage": "organize",
    60→        "report": report,
    61→        "cited_ids": [],
    62→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
    63→        "issue_count": issue_count,
    64→    }
    65→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
    66→        stages["organize"]["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
    67→        stages["organize"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
    68→    return cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "organize")
    69→
    70→
    71→def record_enrich_stage(
    72→    stages: dict,
    73→    *,

    75→    shallow_count: int,
    76→    existing_stage: dict | None,
    77→    is_reuse: bool,

    80→        "stage": "enrich",

    82→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
    83→        "shallow_count": shallow_count,
    84→    }

    86→        stages["enrich"]["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
    87→        stages["enrich"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")

   100→        "report": report,

   104→        stages["sense-check"]["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
   105→        stages["sense-check"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
   106→    return cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "sense-check")
   107→

   109→def record_confirm_existing_completion(
```

```
1→"""Observe stage command flow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     8→from desloppify.base.output.user_message import print_user_message
     9→
    10→from ..stage_queue import (
    11→    cascade_clear_dispositions,
    12→    has_triage_in_queue,
    13→    inject_triage_stages,
    14→    print_cascade_clear_feedback,
    15→)
    16→from ..lifecycle import TriageLifecycleDeps, ensure_triage_started
    17→from ..services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    18→from .flow_helpers import validate_stage_report_length
    19→from .records import record_observe_stage, resolve_reusable_report
    20→from .rendering import _print_observe_report_requirement
    21→
    22→
    23→def cmd_stage_observe(
    24→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    25→    *,
    26→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
    27→    has_triage_in_queue_fn=has_triage_in_queue,
    28→    inject_triage_stages_fn=inject_triage_stages,
    29→) -> None:
    30→    """Record the OBSERVE stage: agent analyses themes and root causes."""
    31→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
    32→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
    33→
    34→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
    35→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
    36→    state = runtime.state
    37→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
    38→
    39→    if not has_triage_in_queue_fn(plan):
    40→        start_outcome = ensure_triage_started(
    41→            plan,
    42→            services=resolved_services,
    43→            state=state,
    44→            attestation=attestation,
    45→            start_message="  Planning mode auto-started (6 stages queued).",
    46→            deps=TriageLifecycleDeps(
    47→                has_triage_in_queue=has_triage_in_queue_fn,
    48→                inject_triage_stages=inject_triage_stages_fn,
    49→            ),
    50→        )
    51→        if start_outcome.status == "blocked":
    52→            return
    53→
    54→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
    55→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
    56→    existing_stage = stages.get("observe")
    57→
    58→    report, is_reuse = resolve_reusable_report(report, existing_stage)
    59→    if not report:
    60→        _print_observe_report_requirement()
    61→        return
    62→
    63→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
    64→    issue_count = len(si.open_issues)
    65→    if issue_count == 0:
    66→        cleared = record_observe_stage(
    67→            stages,
    68→            report=report,
    69→            issue_count=0,
    70→            cited_ids=[],
    71→            existing_stage=existing_stage,
    72→            is_reuse=is_reuse,
    73→        )
    74→        resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
    75→        print(colorize("  Observe stage recorded (no issues to analyse).", "green"))
    76→        if is_reuse:
    77→            print(colorize("  Observe data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
    78→            if cleared:
    79→                print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
    80→        return
    81→
    82→    if not validate_stage_report_length(
    83→        report=report,
    84→        issue_count=issue_count,
    85→        guidance="  Describe themes, root causes, contradictions, and how issues relate.",
    86→    ):
    87→        return
    88→
    89→    from .evidence_parsing import (
    90→        format_evidence_failures,
    91→        parse_observe_evidence,
    92→        resolve_short_hash_to_full_id,
    93→        validate_observe_evidence,
    94→    )
    95→
    96→    valid_ids = set(si.open_issues.keys())
    97→    cited = resolved_services.extract_issue_citations(report, valid_ids)
    98→    evidence = parse_observe_evidence(report, valid_ids)
    99→    evidence_failures = validate_observe_evidence(evidence, issue_count)
   100→    blocking = [failure for failure in evidence_failures if failure.blocking]
   101→    advisory = [failure for failure in evidence_failures if not failure.blocking]
   102→    if blocking:
   103→        print(colorize(format_evidence_failures(blocking, stage_label="observe"), "red"))
   104→        return
   105→    if advisory:
   106→        print(colorize(format_evidence_failures(advisory, stage_label="observe"), "yellow"))
   107→
   108→    assessments = [
   109→        {
   110→            "hash": entry.issue_hash,
   111→            "verdict": entry.verdict,
   112→            "verdict_reasoning": entry.verdict_reasoning,
   113→            "files_read": entry.files_read,
   114→            "recommendation": entry.recommendation,
   115→        }
   116→        for entry in evidence.entries
   117→    ]
   118→
   119→    # On fresh observe run, cascade-clear dispositions and undo auto-skips
   120→    if not is_reuse:
   121→        from ..confirmations.basic import _undo_observe_auto_skips
   122→
   123→        _undo_observe_auto_skips(plan, meta)
   124→        cascade_clear_dispositions(meta, "observe")
   125→
   126→    # Populate issue_dispositions from assessments using collision-aware resolution
   127→    dispositions: dict[str, dict] = {}
   128→    for entry in evidence.entries:
   129→        full_id = resolve_short_hash_to_full_id(entry.issue_hash, valid_ids)
   130→        if full_id:
   131→            dispositions[full_id] = {
   132→                "verdict": entry.verdict,
   133→                "verdict_reasoning": entry.verdict_reasoning,
   134→                "files_read": entry.files_read,
   135→                "recommendation": entry.recommendation,
   136→            }
   137→    meta["issue_dispositions"] = dispositions
   138→
   139→    cleared = record_observe_stage(
   140→        stages,
   141→        report=report,
   142→        issue_count=issue_count,
   143→        cited_ids=sorted(cited),
   144→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   145→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   146→        assessments=assessments,
   147→    )
   148→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   149→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   150→        plan,
   151→        "triage_observe",
   152→        actor="user",
   153→        detail={"issue_count": issue_count, "cited_ids": sorted(cited), "reuse": is_reuse},
   154→    )
   155→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   156→
   157→    print(colorize(f"  Observe stage recorded: {issue_count} issues analysed.", "green"))
   158→    if is_reuse:
   159→        print(colorize("  Observe data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   160→        if cleared:
   161→            print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   162→        return
   163→
   164→    print(colorize("  Now confirm your analysis.", "yellow"))
   165→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm observe", "dim"))
   166→    print_user_message(
   167→        "Observe recorded. Before confirming — did the subagent"
   168→        " verify every issue with code reads? Check: are there"
   169→        " specific file/line citations in the report, or just"
   170→        " restated issue titles? Each issue needs a verdict:"
   171→        " genuine / false positive / exaggerated / not-worth-it. Don't confirm"
   172→        " until the analysis is backed by actual code evidence."
   173→    )
   174→
   175→_cmd_stage_observe = cmd_stage_observe
   176→
   177→
   178→__all__ = ["_cmd_stage_observe", "cmd_stage_observe"]
   179→
```

```
    22→
    23→def _noop_log(_msg: str) -> None:
    24→    """Default run-log sink when the caller doesn't provide one."""
    25→
    26→
    27→def _merge_observe_outputs(
    28→    batch_outputs: list[tuple[list[str], Path]],
    29→) -> str:
    30→    """Concatenate batch outputs with dimension headers into single observe report."""
    31→    parts: list[str] = []
    32→    for dims, output_file in batch_outputs:
    33→        header = f"## Dimensions: {', '.join(dims)}"
    34→        content = ""
    35→        if output_file.exists():
    36→            try:
    37→                content = output_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").strip()
    38→            except OSError:
    39→                content = "(batch output missing)"
    40→        if not content:
    41→            content = "(batch produced no output)"
    42→        parts.append(f"{header}\n\n{content}")
    43→    return "\n\n---\n\n".join(parts)
    44→
    45→
    46→def run_observe(
    47→    *,
    48→    si,
    49→    repo_root: Path,
    50→    prompts_dir: Path,
    51→    output_dir: Path,
    52→    logs_dir: Path,
    53→    timeout_seconds: int,
    54→    dry_run: bool = False,
    55→    append_run_log=None,
    56→) -> TriageStageRunResult:
    57→    """Run observe stage via codex subprocess batches."""
    58→    _log = append_run_log or _noop_log
    59→
    60→    batches = group_issues_into_observe_batches(si)
    61→    total = len(batches)
    62→    print(colorize(f"\n  Observe: splitting into {total} parallel batches.", "bold"))
    63→    _log(f"observe-parallel batches={total}")
    64→
    65→    tasks: dict[int, Callable[[], TriageStageRunResult]] = {}
    66→    batch_meta: list[tuple[list[str], Path]] = []
    67→
    68→    for i, (dims, issues_subset) in enumerate(batches):
    69→        prompt = build_observe_batch_prompt(
    70→            batch_index=i + 1,
    71→            total_batches=total,
    72→            dimension_group=dims,
    73→            issues_subset=issues_subset,
    74→            repo_root=repo_root,
    75→        )
    76→        prompt_file = prompts_dir / f"observe_batch_{i}.md"
    77→        safe_write_text(prompt_file, prompt)
    78→
    79→        output_file = output_dir / f"observe_batch_{i}.raw.txt"
    80→        log_file = logs_dir / f"observe_batch_{i}.log"
    81→        batch_meta.append((dims, output_file))
    82→
    83→        if not dry_run:
    84→            tasks[i] = partial(
    85→                run_triage_stage,
    86→                prompt=prompt,
    87→                repo_root=repo_root,
    88→                output_file=output_file,
    89→                log_file=log_file,
    90→                timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
    91→                validate_output_fn=_output_file_has_text,
    92→            )
    93→
    94→        dim_list = ", ".join(dims)
    95→        print(colorize(f"    Batch {i + 1}: {len(issues_subset)} issues ({dim_list})", "dim"))
    96→        _log(f"observe-batch batch={i + 1} issues={len(issues_subset)} dims={dim_list}")
    97→
    98→    if dry_run:
    99→        print(colorize("  [dry-run] Would execute parallel observe batches.", "dim"))
   100→        return TriageStageRunResult(exit_code=0, reason="dry_run", dry_run=True)
   101→
   102→    def _heartbeat(event: BatchProgressEvent) -> None:
   103→        details = event.details or {}
   104→        active = details.get("active_batches", [])
   105→        elapsed_map = details.get("elapsed_seconds", {})
   106→        if active:
   107→            parts = [f"#{i + 1}:{int(elapsed_map.get(i, 0))}s" for i in active[:6]]
   108→            print(colorize(f"    Observe heartbeat: {len(active)}/{total} active ({', '.join(parts)})", "dim"))
   109→
   110→    def _batch_label(idx: int) -> str:
   111→        return f"batch {idx + 1}/{total}"
   112→
   113→    failures = run_parallel_batches(
   114→        tasks=tasks,
   115→        stage_label="Observe",
   116→        batch_label_fn=_batch_label,
   117→        append_run_log=_log,
   118→        heartbeat_seconds=15.0,
   119→        heartbeat_printer=_heartbeat,
   120→    )
   121→
   122→    if failures:
   123→        print(colorize(f"  Observe: {len(failures)} batch(es) failed: {failures}", "red"))
   124→        for idx in failures:
   125→            log_file = logs_dir / f"observe_batch_{idx}.log"
   126→            print(colorize(f"    Check log: {log_file}", "dim"))
   127→        _log(f"observe-parallel-failed failures={failures}")
   128→        return TriageStageRunResult(
   129→            exit_code=1,
   130→            reason="parallel_execution_failed",
   131→        )
   132→
   133→    merged = _merge_observe_outputs(batch_meta)
   134→    print(colorize(f"  Observe: merged {total} batch outputs ({len(merged)} chars).", "green"))
   135→    _log(f"observe-parallel-done merged_chars={len(merged)}")
   136→    return TriageStageRunResult(
   137→        exit_code=0,
   138→        merged_output=merged,
   139→    )
   140→
   141→
   142→__all__ = ["run_observe"]
   143→
```

```
     2→

    20→from ..observe_batches import observe_dimension_breakdown
    21→from .reflect_accounting import validate_reflect_accounting

    23→
    24→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    25→class AutoConfirmStageRequest:
    26→    """Configuration for one fold-confirm stage auto-confirmation."""
    27→
    28→    stage_name: str
    29→    stage_label: str
    30→    blocked_heading: str
    31→    confirm_cmd: str
    32→    inline_hint: str
    33→    dimensions: list[str] | None = None
    34→    cluster_names: list[str] | None = None
    35→
    36→
    37→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    38→class ReflectAutoConfirmDeps:
    39→    """Dependency bundle for reflect auto-confirm flows."""
    40→
    41→    triage_input: object | None = None
    42→    command_runtime_fn: object | None = None
    43→    collect_triage_input_fn: object = collect_triage_input
    44→    detect_recurring_patterns_fn: object = detect_recurring_patterns
    45→    save_plan_fn: object | None = None
    46→
    47→
    48→def require_prerequisite(
    49→    stages: dict,
    50→    *,
    51→    flow: str,
    52→    messages: dict[str, tuple[str, str]],
    53→) -> bool:
    54→    """Print consistent prerequisite guidance for one triage flow."""
    55→    flow_stage = flow.removeprefix("complete:")
    56→    progress = compute_triage_progress(stages)
    57→    if flow_stage in stages or progress.current_stage == flow_stage:
    58→        return True
    59→
    60→    for prerequisite in TRIAGE_STAGE_PREREQUISITES.get(flow_stage, ()):
    61→        stage_record = stages.get(prerequisite.stage_name)
    62→        if stage_record is None:
    63→            blocked_heading, command_hint = messages[prerequisite.stage_name]
    64→            print(colorize(blocked_heading, "red"))
    65→            print(colorize(command_hint, "dim"))
    66→            return False
    67→        if prerequisite.require_confirmation and not stage_record.get("confirmed_at"):
    68→            blocked_heading, command_hint = messages[prerequisite.stage_name]
    69→            print(colorize(blocked_heading, "red"))
    70→            print(colorize(command_hint, "dim"))
    71→            return False
    72→
    73→    if progress.blocked_reason:
    74→        print(colorize(f"  {progress.blocked_reason}", "red"))
    75→        if progress.next_command:
    76→            print(colorize(f"  Run: {progress.next_command}", "dim"))
    77→        return False
    78→
    79→    blocked_heading, command_hint = next(iter(messages.values()))
    80→    print(colorize(blocked_heading, "red"))
    81→    print(colorize(command_hint, "dim"))
    82→    return False
    83→
    84→
    85→def confirm_stage(
    86→    *,
    87→    plan: dict,
    88→    stage_record: dict,
    89→    attestation: str | None,
    90→    request: AutoConfirmStageRequest,
    91→    save_plan_fn=None,
    92→    utc_now_fn=None,
    93→) -> bool:
    94→    """Auto-confirm one recorded stage with a validated attestation."""
    95→    resolved_save_plan = save_plan_fn or save_plan
    96→    resolved_utc_now = utc_now_fn or utc_now
    97→    if stage_record.get("confirmed_at"):
    98→        return True
    99→    if not attestation or len(attestation.strip()) < MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
   100→        print(colorize(f"  {request.blocked_heading}", "red"))
   101→        print(colorize(f"  Run: {request.confirm_cmd}", "dim"))
   102→        print(colorize(f"  {request.inline_hint}", "dim"))
   103→        return False
   104→
   105→    confirmed_text = attestation.strip()
   106→    validation_err = validate_attestation(
   107→        confirmed_text,
   108→        request.stage_name,
   109→        dimensions=request.dimensions,
   110→        cluster_names=request.cluster_names,
   111→    )
   112→    if validation_err:
   113→        print(colorize(f"  {validation_err}", "red"))
   114→        return False
   115→
   116→    stage_record["confirmed_at"] = resolved_utc_now()
   117→    stage_record["confirmed_text"] = confirmed_text
   118→    resolved_save_plan(plan)
   119→    print(colorize(f"  ✓ {request.stage_label} auto-confirmed via --attestation.", "green"))
   120→    return True
   121→
   122→
   123→def auto_confirm_observe_if_attested(
   124→    *,
   125→    plan: dict,
   126→    stages: dict,
   127→    attestation: str | None,
   128→    triage_input,
   129→    save_plan_fn=None,
   130→    utc_now_fn=None,
   131→) -> bool:
   132→    """Auto-confirm observe inline when the report already exists."""
   133→    observe_stage = stages.get("observe")
   134→    if observe_stage is None:
   135→        return False
   136→    _by_dim, dim_names = observe_dimension_breakdown(triage_input)
   137→    return confirm_stage(
   138→        plan=plan,
   139→        stage_record=observe_stage,
   140→        attestation=attestation,
   141→        request=AutoConfirmStageRequest(
   142→            stage_name="observe",
   143→            stage_label="Observe",
   144→            blocked_heading="Cannot reflect: observe stage not confirmed.",
   145→            confirm_cmd="desloppify plan triage --confirm observe",
   146→            inline_hint="Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm observe inline.",
   147→            dimensions=dim_names,
   148→        ),
   149→        save_plan_fn=save_plan_fn,
   150→        utc_now_fn=utc_now_fn,
```

```
    59→    TriageStartDecision,
    60→    decide_triage_start,
    61→)
    62→from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.context import has_objective_backlog
    63→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    64→
    65→
    66→def triage_phase_banner(
    67→    plan: PlanModel,
    68→    state: dict | None = None,
    69→    *,
    70→    snapshot: TriageSnapshot | None = None,
    71→) -> str:
    72→    """Return a banner string describing triage status."""
    73→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    74→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    75→    run_hint = (
    76→        f"Run: {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX} "
    77→        f"(or {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE})"
    78→    )
    79→    resolved_state = state or {}
    80→    resolved_snapshot = snapshot or build_triage_snapshot(plan, resolved_state)
    81→
    82→    if not resolved_snapshot.has_triage_in_queue:
    83→        undispositioned = len(resolved_snapshot.undispositioned_ids)
    84→        if undispositioned:
    85→            return (
    86→                "TRIAGE RECOVERY NEEDED — "
    87→                f"{undispositioned} review issue(s) still need cluster/skip dispositions. "
    88→                f"{run_hint}"
    89→            )
    90→        if resolved_snapshot.is_triage_stale or meta.get("triage_recommended"):
    91→            return (
    92→                "TRIAGE RECOMMENDED — review issues changed since last triage. "
    93→                f"{run_hint}"
    94→            )
    95→        return ""
    96→
    97→    if resolved_snapshot.requires_plan_before_execution:
    98→        if not resolved_snapshot.triage_has_run:
    99→            return (
   100→                "TRIAGE REQUIRED BEFORE EXECUTION — "
   101→                f"{resolved_snapshot.total_in_scope} imported review issue(s) still need an organized plan. "
   102→                f"{run_hint}"
   103→            )
   104→        undispositioned = len(resolved_snapshot.undispositioned_ids)
   105→        if undispositioned:
   106→            return (
   107→                "TRIAGE REQUIRED BEFORE EXECUTION — "
   108→                f"{undispositioned} review issue(s) still need cluster/skip dispositions. "
   109→                f"{run_hint}"
   110→            )
   111→        if resolved_snapshot.is_triage_stale:
   112→            return (
   113→                "TRIAGE REQUIRED BEFORE EXECUTION — "
   114→                f"{len(resolved_snapshot.new_since_triage_ids)} new review issue(s) changed since the last triage run. "
   115→                f"{run_hint}"
   116→            )
   117→        if resolved_state and has_objective_backlog(resolved_state, None):
   118→            return (
   119→                "TRIAGE REQUIRED BEFORE EXECUTION — queued review planning is blocking detector-first work. "
   120→                f"{run_hint}"
   121→            )
   122→        return (
   123→            "TRIAGE MODE — review issues need analysis before fixing. "
   124→            f"{run_hint}"
   125→        )
   126→    progress = resolved_snapshot.progress
   127→    if progress.completed_count:
   128→        return (
   129→            f"TRIAGE MODE ({progress.completed_count}/6 stages recorded) — "
   130→            f"complete all stages to exit. {run_hint}"
   131→        )
   132→    return (
   133→        "TRIAGE MODE — review issues need analysis before fixing. "
   134→        f"{run_hint}"
   135→    )
   136→
   137→__all__ = [
   138→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ADD",
   139→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_CREATE",
```

```
1→"""Shared helpers for triage stage confirmation flows."""
     2→

     8→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize

    10→
    11→from ..stage_queue import purge_triage_stage
    12→
    13→_STAGE_LABELS = {
    14→    "observe": "Observe",
    15→    "reflect": "Reflect",
    16→    "organize": "Organize",
    17→    "enrich": "Enrich",
    18→    "sense-check": "Sense-check",
    19→}
    20→
    21→
    22→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    23→class StageConfirmationRequest:
    24→    """Input contract for shared stage-confirmation behavior."""
    25→
    26→    stage: str
    27→    attestation: str | None
    28→    min_attestation_len: int
    29→    command_hint: str
    30→    validation_stage: str
    31→    validate_attestation_fn: Callable[..., str | None]
    32→    log_action: str
    33→    validation_kwargs: dict[str, object] | None = None
    34→    log_detail: dict[str, object] | None = None
    35→    not_satisfied_hint: str | None = None
    36→
    37→
    38→def ensure_stage_is_confirmable(stages: dict, *, stage: str) -> bool:
    39→    """Validate stage presence/confirmation status before confirm flow runs."""
    40→    if stage not in stages:
    41→        print(colorize(f"  Cannot confirm: {stage} stage not recorded.", "red"))
    42→        print(colorize(f'  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage {stage} --report "..."', "dim"))
    43→        return False
    44→    if stages[stage].get("confirmed_at"):
    45→        label = _STAGE_LABELS.get(stage, stage.title())
    46→        print(colorize(f"  {label} stage already confirmed.", "green"))
    47→        return False
    48→    return True
    49→
    50→
    51→def finalize_stage_confirmation(
    52→    *,
    53→    plan: dict,
    54→    stages: dict,
    55→    request: StageConfirmationRequest,
    56→    services,
    57→) -> bool:
    58→    """Apply shared attestation validation + state mutation + log/save flow."""
    59→    attestation_text = (request.attestation or "").strip()
    60→    if len(attestation_text) < request.min_attestation_len:
    61→        if attestation_text:
    62→            print(
    63→                colorize(
    64→                    (
    65→                        "\n  Attestation too short "
    66→                        f"({len(attestation_text)} chars, min {request.min_attestation_len})."
    67→                    ),
    68→                    "red",
    69→                )
    70→            )
    71→        print(colorize("\n  If satisfied, confirm:", "dim"))
    72→        print(colorize(f"    {request.command_hint}", "dim"))
    73→        if request.not_satisfied_hint:
    74→            print(colorize(f"  {request.not_satisfied_hint}", "dim"))
    75→        return False
    76→
    77→    err = request.validate_attestation_fn(
    78→        attestation_text,
    79→        request.validation_stage,
    80→        **(request.validation_kwargs or {}),
    81→    )
    82→    if err:
    83→        print(colorize(f"\n  {err}", "red"))
    84→        return False
    85→
    86→    stages[request.stage]["confirmed_at"] = utc_now()
    87→    stages[request.stage]["confirmed_text"] = attestation_text
    88→    purge_triage_stage(plan, request.stage)
    89→
    90→    detail = {"attestation": attestation_text}
    91→    if request.log_detail:
    92→        detail.update(request.log_detail)
    93→    services.append_log_entry(
    94→        plan,
    95→        request.log_action,
    96→        actor="user",
    97→        detail=detail,
    98→    )
    99→    services.save_plan(plan)
   100→    label = _STAGE_LABELS.get(request.stage, request.stage.title())
   101→    print(colorize(f'  ✓ {label} confirmed: "{attestation_text}"', "green"))
   102→    return True
   103→
   104→
   105→__all__ = [
   106→    "StageConfirmationRequest",
   107→    "ensure_stage_is_confirmable",
   108→    "finalize_stage_confirmation",
   109→]
   110→
```

```
    64→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    65→    services: TriageServices,
    66→) -> None:
    67→    from ..stages.commands import cmd_stage_reflect
    68→
    69→    record_args = argparse.Namespace(
    70→        stage="reflect",
    71→        report=report,
    72→        state=getattr(args, "state", None),
    73→    )
    74→    cmd_stage_reflect(record_args, services=services)
    75→
    76→
    77→def _record_sense_check_report(
    78→    report: str,
    79→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    80→    services: TriageServices,
    81→) -> None:
    82→    from ..stages.commands import cmd_stage_sense_check
    83→
    84→    record_args = argparse.Namespace(
    85→        stage="sense-check",
    86→        report=report,
    87→        state=getattr(args, "state", None),
    88→    )
    89→    cmd_stage_sense_check(record_args, services=services)
    90→
    91→
    92→DEFAULT_STAGE_HANDLERS: dict[str, StageHandler] = {
    93→    "observe": StageHandler(
    94→        run_parallel=lambda context: run_observe(
    95→            si=context.triage_input,
    96→            repo_root=context.repo_root,
    97→            prompts_dir=context.prompts_dir,
    98→            output_dir=context.output_dir,
    99→            logs_dir=context.logs_dir,
   100→            timeout_seconds=context.timeout_seconds,
   101→            dry_run=context.dry_run,
   102→            append_run_log=context.append_run_log,
   103→        ),
   104→        record_report=_record_observe_report,
   105→    ),
   106→    "reflect": StageHandler(
   107→        record_report=_record_reflect_report,
   108→    ),
   109→    "organize": StageHandler(
   110→        prompt_mode="self_record",
   111→    ),
   112→    "enrich": StageHandler(
   113→        prompt_mode="self_record",
   114→    ),
   115→    "sense-check": StageHandler(
   116→        run_parallel=lambda context: run_sense_check(
   117→            plan=dict(context.plan),
   118→            repo_root=context.repo_root,
   119→            prompts_dir=context.prompts_dir,
   120→            output_dir=context.output_dir,
   121→            logs_dir=context.logs_dir,
   122→            timeout_seconds=context.timeout_seconds,
   123→            dry_run=context.dry_run,
   124→            cli_command=context.cli_command,
   125→            apply_updates=True,
   126→            reload_plan=context.services.load_plan,
   127→            append_run_log=context.append_run_log,
   128→        ),
   129→        record_report=_record_sense_check_report,
   130→    ),
   131→}
   132→
   133→
   134→@dataclass(frozen=True)
   135→class StageExecutionDependencies:
   136→    """Dependency container for stage execution to support focused patching in tests."""
   137→
   138→    build_stage_prompt: Callable[..., str]
   139→    run_triage_stage: Callable[..., TriageStageRunResult]
   140→    read_stage_output: Callable[[Path], str]
   141→    analyze_reflect_issue_accounting: Callable[..., tuple[set[str], list[str], list[str]]]
   142→    validate_reflect_issue_accounting: Callable[
   143→        ...,
   144→        tuple[bool, set[str], list[str], list[str]],
   145→    ]
   146→
   147→
   148→@dataclass(frozen=True)
   149→class StageExecutionResult:
   150→    """Explicit outcome model for one stage-execution step."""
   151→
   152→    status: str
   153→    payload: dict[str, Any]
   154→    used_parallel: bool = False
   155→    output_file: Path | None = None
   156→    elapsed_seconds: int | None = None
   157→
   158→
   159→def default_stage_execution_dependencies() -> StageExecutionDependencies:
   160→    """Construct the default stage execution dependency set."""
   161→    return StageExecutionDependencies(
   162→        build_stage_prompt=build_stage_prompt,
   163→        run_triage_stage=run_triage_stage,
   164→        read_stage_output=read_stage_output,
   165→        analyze_reflect_issue_accounting=analyze_reflect_issue_accounting,
   166→        validate_reflect_issue_accounting=validate_reflect_accounting,
   167→    )
   168→
   169→
   170→def stage_report_recorded(plan: Mapping[str, Any], stage: str) -> bool:
   171→    """True when the plan contains a persisted report for the given stage."""
   172→    return bool(
   173→        plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   174→        .get("triage_stages", {})
   175→        .get(stage, {})
   176→        .get("report", "")
   177→    )
   178→
   179→
   180→def preflight_stage(
   181→    *,
   182→    stage: str,
   183→    prompt_mode: PromptMode = "output_only",
   184→    plan: Mapping[str, Any],
   185→    triage_input: Any,
   186→    dry_run: bool,
   187→    append_run_log: Callable[[str], None],
   188→    validate_reflect_issue_accounting: Callable[
   189→        ...,
   190→        tuple[bool, set[str], list[str], list[str]],
   191→    ],
   192→) -> tuple[bool, str | None]:
   193→    """Fail fast when a requested stage has invalid upstream prerequisites."""
   194→    # Dry-run previews do not persist upstream stage reports, so preflight checks that
   195→    # require recorded reflect/enrich state would otherwise fail by construction.
   196→    if dry_run and stage in {"organize", "sense-check"}:
   197→        return True, None
   198→
   199→    if stage == "sense-check":
   200→        stages = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("triage_stages", {})
   201→        progress = compute_triage_progress(stages)
   202→        if "sense-check" in stages or progress.current_stage == "sense-check":
   203→            return True, None
   204→        reason = "enrich_not_confirmed"
   205→        append_run_log(f"stage-preflight-failed stage={stage} reason={reason}")
   206→        return False, reason
   207→
   208→    if stage != "organize":
   209→        return True, None
   210→    stages = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("triage_stages", {})
   211→    reflect_report = str(stages.get("reflect", {}).get("report", ""))
   212→    accounting_ok, _cited, missing_ids, duplicate_ids = validate_reflect_issue_accounting(
   213→        report=reflect_report,
   214→        valid_ids=set(getattr(triage_input, "open_issues", {}).keys()),
   215→    )
   216→    if not accounting_ok:
   217→        reason_parts: list[str] = []
   218→        if missing_ids:
   219→            reason_parts.append(f"missing={len(missing_ids)}")
   220→        if duplicate_ids:
   221→            reason_parts.append(f"duplicates={len(duplicate_ids)}")
   222→        reason = "reflect_accounting_invalid"
   223→        if reason_parts:
   224→            reason = f"{reason}({' '.join(reason_parts)})"
   225→        append_run_log(f"stage-preflight-failed stage={stage} reason={reason}")
   226→        return False, reason
   227→
   228→    # Self-record stages mutate plan state from within the subagent, so their
   229→    # postconditions cannot be required before launch. The organize command
   230→    # itself validates ledger/disposition alignment when it records the stage.
   231→    if prompt_mode == "self_record":
   232→        return True, None
   233→
   234→    # Output-only organize paths may rely on pre-applied plan mutations.
   235→    ledger_mismatches = validate_organize_against_reflect_ledger(
   236→        plan=dict(plan), stages=stages,
   237→    )
   238→    if ledger_mismatches:
   239→        mismatch_types = set()
   240→        for m in ledger_mismatches:
   241→            mismatch_types.add(m.expected_decision)
   242→        reason = f"reflect_ledger_mismatch(count={len(ledger_mismatches)} types={','.join(sorted(mismatch_types))})"
   243→        append_run_log(f"stage-preflight-failed stage={stage} reason={reason}")
   244→        return False, reason
   245→
   246→    return True, None
   247→
   248→
   249→def build_reflect_repair_prompt(
   250→    *,
   251→    triage_input: Any,
   252→    prior_reports: Mapping[str, str],
   253→    repo_root: Path,
   254→    cli_command: str,
   255→    original_report: str,
   256→    missing_ids: list[str],
   257→    duplicate_ids: list[str],
   258→    build_stage_prompt_fn: Callable[..., str],
   259→    stages_data: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
   260→) -> str:
   261→    """Build a targeted retry prompt for a reflect report that failed accounting."""
   262→    missing_short = ", ".join(issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1] for issue_id in missing_ids) or "none"
   263→    duplicate_short = (
   264→        ", ".join(issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1] for issue_id in duplicate_ids) or "none"
   265→    )
   266→    base_prompt = build_stage_prompt_fn(
   267→        "reflect",
   268→        triage_input,
   269→        dict(prior_reports),
   270→        repo_root=repo_root,
   271→        mode="output_only",
   272→        cli_command=cli_command,
   273→        stages_data=stages_data,
   274→    )
   275→    return "\n\n".join(
   276→        [
   277→            base_prompt,
   278→            "## Repair Pass",
   279→            "Your previous reflect report failed the exact-hash accounting check.",
   280→            f"Missing hashes: {missing_short}",
   281→            f"Duplicated hashes: {duplicate_short}",
   282→            "Rewrite the FULL reflect report so it passes validation.",
   283→            "Requirements for this repair:",
   284→            "- Start with a `## Coverage Ledger` section.",
   285→            '- Use one ledger line per issue hash: `- abcd1234 -> cluster "name"` or `- abcd1234 -> skip "reason"`.',
   286→            "- Mention every required hash exactly once in that ledger.",
   287→            "- Do not mention hashes anywhere else in the report.",
   288→            "- Preserve the same strategy unless fixing the missing/duplicate hashes forces a small adjustment.",
   289→            "- Output only the corrected reflect report.",
   290→            "## Previous Reflect Report",
   291→            original_report,
   292→        ]
   293→    )
   294→
   295→
   296→def repair_reflect_report_if_needed(
   297→    *,
   298→    report: str,
   299→    triage_input: Any,
   300→    prior_reports: Mapping[str, str],
   301→    repo_root: Path,
   302→    prompts_dir: Path,
   303→    output_dir: Path,
   304→    logs_dir: Path,
   305→    cli_command: str,
   306→    timeout_seconds: int,
   307→    append_run_log: Callable[[str], None],
   308→    dependencies: StageExecutionDependencies,
   309→    stages_data: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
   310→) -> tuple[str | None, str | None]:
   311→    """Retry reflect once with a targeted repair prompt when accounting is invalid."""
   312→    _cited, missing_ids, duplicate_ids = dependencies.analyze_reflect_issue_accounting(
   313→        report=report,
   314→        valid_ids=set(getattr(triage_input, "open_issues", {}).keys()),
   315→    )
   316→    if not missing_ids and not duplicate_ids:
   317→        return report, None
   318→
   319→    print(colorize("  Reflect: repairing missing/duplicate hash accounting...", "yellow"))
   320→    append_run_log(
   321→        "stage-reflect-repair-start "
   322→        f"missing={len(missing_ids)} duplicates={len(duplicate_ids)}"
   323→    )
   324→
   325→    repair_prompt = build_reflect_repair_prompt(
   326→        triage_input=triage_input,
   327→        prior_reports=prior_reports,
   328→        repo_root=repo_root,
   329→        cli_command=cli_command,
   330→        original_report=report,
   331→        missing_ids=missing_ids,
   332→        duplicate_ids=duplicate_ids,
   333→        build_stage_prompt_fn=dependencies.build_stage_prompt,
   334→        stages_data=stages_data,
   335→    )
   336→    repair_prompt_file = prompts_dir / "reflect.repair.md"
   337→    repair_output_file = output_dir / "reflect.repair.raw.txt"
   338→    repair_log_file = logs_dir / "reflect.repair.log"
   339→    safe_write_text(repair_prompt_file, repair_prompt)
   340→    stage_result = dependencies.run_triage_stage(
   341→        prompt=repair_prompt,
   342→        repo_root=repo_root,
   343→        output_file=repair_output_file,
   344→        log_file=repair_log_file,
   345→        timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
   346→    )
   347→    append_run_log(f"stage-reflect-repair-done code={stage_result.exit_code}")
   348→    if not stage_result.ok:
   349→        return None, f"reflect_repair_failed_exit_{stage_result.exit_code}"
   350→
   351→    repaired_report = dependencies.read_stage_output(repair_output_file)
   352→    if not repaired_report:
   353→        return None, "reflect_repair_empty_output"
   354→
   355→    _cited, missing_after, duplicates_after = dependencies.analyze_reflect_issue_accounting(
   356→        report=repaired_report,
   357→        valid_ids=set(getattr(triage_input, "open_issues", {}).keys()),
   358→    )
   359→    if missing_after or duplicates_after:
   360→        return None, "reflect_repair_invalid"
   361→
   362→    print(colorize("  Reflect: repair pass fixed issue accounting.", "green"))
   363→    append_run_log("stage-reflect-repair-success")
   364→    return repaired_report, None
   365→
   366→
   367→def _execute_parallel_stage(
   368→    *,
   369→    context: StageRunContext,
   370→    stage: str,
   371→    handler: StageHandler | None,
   372→) -> StageExecutionResult:
   373→    """Execute optional parallel stage path."""
   374→    if handler is None or handler.run_parallel is None:
   375→        return StageExecutionResult(status="ready", payload={})
   376→
   377→    parallel_result = handler.run_parallel(context)
   378→    if parallel_result.status == "dry_run":
   379→        return StageExecutionResult(
   380→            status="dry_run",
   381→            payload={"status": "dry_run"},
   382→            used_parallel=True,
   383→        )
   384→
   385→    if parallel_result.ok and parallel_result.merged_output:
   386→        if handler.record_report is not None:
   387→            handler.record_report(parallel_result.merged_output, context.args, context.services)
   388→            return StageExecutionResult(status="ready", payload={}, used_parallel=True)
   389→        return StageExecutionResult(status="ready", payload={})
   390→
   391→    if parallel_result.ok:
   392→        return StageExecutionResult(status="ready", payload={})
   393→
   394→    elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - context.stage_start)
   395→    error_reason = parallel_result.reason or "parallel_execution_failed"
   396→    print(colorize(f"  {stage.capitalize()}: parallel execution failed. Aborting.", "red"))
   397→    context.append_run_log(
   398→        f"stage-failed stage={stage} elapsed={elapsed}s reason={error_reason}"
   399→    )
   400→    return StageExecutionResult(
   401→        status="failed",
   402→        payload={
   403→            "status": "failed",
   404→            "elapsed_seconds": elapsed,
   405→            "error": error_reason,
   406→        },
   407→        used_parallel=True,
   408→        elapsed_seconds=elapsed,
   409→    )
   410→
   411→
   412→def _failure_result(
   413→    *,
   414→    stage: str,
   415→    elapsed: int | None,
   416→    error: str,
   417→    append_run_log: Callable[[str], None] | None = None,
   418→    log_event: str = "stage-failed",
   419→    log_reason: str | None = None,
   420→    printed_message: str | None = None,
   421→) -> StageExecutionResult:
   422→    if printed_message is not None:
   423→        print(colorize(printed_message, "red"))
   424→    if append_run_log is not None:
   425→        log_parts = [f"{log_event} stage={stage}"]
   426→        if elapsed is not None:
   427→            log_parts.append(f"elapsed={elapsed}s")
   428→        log_parts.append(f"reason={log_reason or error}")
   429→        append_run_log(" ".join(log_parts))
   430→    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
   431→        "status": "failed",
   432→        "error": error,
   433→    }
   434→    if elapsed is not None:
   435→        payload["elapsed_seconds"] = elapsed
   436→    return StageExecutionResult(
   437→        status="failed",
   438→        payload=payload,
   439→        elapsed_seconds=elapsed,
   440→    )
   441→
   442→
   443→def _build_subprocess_prompt(
   444→    *,
   445→    context: StageRunContext,
   446→    stage: str,
   447→    prompt_mode: PromptMode,
   448→    dependencies: StageExecutionDependencies,
   449→) -> tuple[str, Mapping[str, Any]]:
   450→    """Build and persist one stage prompt for subprocess execution."""
   451→    stages_data = context.plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("triage_stages", {})
   452→    prompt = dependencies.build_stage_prompt(
   453→        stage,
   454→        context.triage_input,
   455→        dict(context.prior_reports),
   456→        repo_root=context.repo_root,
   457→        mode=prompt_mode,
   458→        cli_command=context.cli_command,
   459→        stages_data=stages_data,
   460→    )
   461→    prompt_file = context.prompts_dir / f"{stage}.md"
   462→    safe_write_text(prompt_file, prompt)
   463→    return prompt, stages_data
   464→
   465→
   466→def _run_subprocess_stage(
   467→    *,
   468→    context: StageRunContext,
   469→    stage: str,
   470→    prompt: str,
   471→    dependencies: StageExecutionDependencies,
   472→) -> StageExecutionResult:
   473→    """Run codex subprocess for one stage (or emit dry-run status)."""
   474→    prompt_file = context.prompts_dir / f"{stage}.md"
   475→    if context.dry_run:
   476→        print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: prompt written to {prompt_file}", "cyan"))
   477→        print(colorize("  [dry-run] Would execute codex subprocess.", "dim"))
   478→        return StageExecutionResult(
   479→            status="dry_run",
   480→            payload={"status": "dry_run"},
   481→        )
   482→
   483→    print(colorize(f"\n  Stage {stage}: launching codex subprocess...", "bold"))
   484→    context.append_run_log(f"stage-subprocess-start stage={stage}")
   485→
   486→    output_file = context.output_dir / f"{stage}.raw.txt"
   487→    log_file = context.logs_dir / f"{stage}.log"
   488→    stage_result = dependencies.run_triage_stage(
   489→        prompt=prompt,
   490→        repo_root=context.repo_root,
   491→        output_file=output_file,
   492→        log_file=log_file,
   493→        timeout_seconds=context.timeout_seconds,
   494→    )
   495→
   496→    elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - context.stage_start)
   497→    context.append_run_log(
   498→        "stage-subprocess-done "
   499→        f"stage={stage} code={stage_result.exit_code} elapsed={elapsed}s"
   500→    )
   501→
   502→    if stage_result.ok:
   503→        return StageExecutionResult(
   504→            status="ready",
   505→            payload={},
   506→            output_file=output_file,
   507→            elapsed_seconds=elapsed,
   508→        )
   509→
   510→    print(
   511→        colorize(
   512→            "  Stage "
   513→            f"{stage}: codex subprocess failed (exit {stage_result.exit_code}).",
   514→            "red",
   515→        )
   516→    )
   517→    print(colorize(f"  Check log: {log_file}", "dim"))
   518→    print(colorize("  Re-run to resume (confirmed stages are skipped).", "dim"))
   519→    context.append_run_log(
   520→        "stage-failed "
   521→        f"stage={stage} elapsed={elapsed}s code={stage_result.exit_code}"
   522→    )
   523→    return StageExecutionResult(
   524→        status="failed",
   525→        payload={
   526→            "status": "failed",
   527→            "exit_code": stage_result.exit_code,
   528→            "elapsed_seconds": elapsed,
   529→        },
   530→        elapsed_seconds=elapsed,
   531→    )
   532→
   533→
   534→def _resolve_subprocess_artifacts(
   535→    subprocess_result: StageExecutionResult,
   536→) -> tuple[Path, int] | None:
   537→    output_file = subprocess_result.output_file
   538→    elapsed = subprocess_result.elapsed_seconds
   539→    if output_file is None or elapsed is None:
   540→        return None
   541→    return output_file, elapsed
   542→
   543→
   544→def _record_stage_report_if_needed(
   545→    *,
   546→    context: StageRunContext,
   547→    stage: str,
   548→    handler: StageHandler | None,
   549→    dependencies: StageExecutionDependencies,
   550→    output_file: Path,
   551→    elapsed: int,
   552→    stages_data: Mapping[str, Any],
   553→) -> StageExecutionResult:
   554→    """Record subprocess output for stages that require orchestrator persistence."""
   555→    if handler is None or handler.record_report is None:
   556→        return StageExecutionResult(status="ready", payload={})
   557→
   558→    report = dependencies.read_stage_output(output_file)
   559→    if not report:
   560→        return _failure_result(
   561→            stage=stage,
   562→            elapsed=elapsed,
   563→            error="empty_stage_output",
   564→            append_run_log=context.append_run_log,
   565→            printed_message=f"  Stage {stage}: output file was empty after subprocess.",
   566→        )
   567→
   568→    if stage == "reflect":
   569→        report, repair_error = repair_reflect_report_if_needed(
   570→            report=report,
   571→            triage_input=context.triage_input,
   572→            prior_reports=context.prior_reports,
   573→            repo_root=context.repo_root,
   574→            prompts_dir=context.prompts_dir,
   575→            output_dir=context.output_dir,
   576→            logs_dir=context.logs_dir,
   577→            cli_command=context.cli_command,
   578→            timeout_seconds=context.timeout_seconds,
   579→            append_run_log=context.append_run_log,
   580→            dependencies=dependencies,
   581→            stages_data=stages_data,
   582→        )
   583→        if repair_error:
   584→            return _failure_result(
   585→                stage=stage,
   586→                elapsed=elapsed,
   587→                error=repair_error,
   588→                append_run_log=context.append_run_log,
   589→                printed_message=f"  Stage {stage}: repair failed ({repair_error}).",
   590→            )
   591→        if not report:
   592→            return _failure_result(
   593→                stage=stage,
   594→                elapsed=elapsed,
   595→                error="reflect_repair_no_report",
   596→                append_run_log=context.append_run_log,
   597→            )
   598→
   599→    handler.record_report(report, context.args, context.services)
   600→    plan_after_record = context.services.load_plan()
   601→    if not stage_report_recorded(plan_after_record, stage):
   602→        return _failure_result(
   603→            stage=stage,
   604→            elapsed=elapsed,
   605→            error="stage_not_recorded",
   606→            append_run_log=context.append_run_log,
   607→            log_event="stage-record-failed",
   608→            printed_message=f"  Stage {stage}: handler completed but did not persist the stage.",
   609→        )
   610→    context.append_run_log(
   611→        f"stage-recorded stage={stage} elapsed={elapsed}s mode=orchestrator"
   612→    )
   613→    return StageExecutionResult(status="ready", payload={})
   614→
   615→
   616→def _run_stage_subprocess_path(
   617→    *,
   618→    context: StageRunContext,
   619→    stage: str,
   620→    handler: StageHandler | None,
   621→    prompt_mode: PromptMode,
   622→    dependencies: StageExecutionDependencies,
   623→) -> StageExecutionResult:
   624→    prompt, stages_data = _build_subprocess_prompt(
   625→        context=context,
   626→        stage=stage,
   627→        prompt_mode=prompt_mode,
   628→        dependencies=dependencies,
   629→    )
   630→    subprocess_result = _run_subprocess_stage(
   631→        context=context,
   632→        stage=stage,
   633→        prompt=prompt,
   634→        dependencies=dependencies,
   635→    )
   636→    if subprocess_result.status != "ready":
   637→        return subprocess_result
   638→
   639→    subprocess_artifacts = _resolve_subprocess_artifacts(subprocess_result)
   640→    if subprocess_artifacts is None:
   641→        return _failure_result(
   642→            stage=stage,
   643→            elapsed=None,
   644→            error="subprocess_output_missing",
   645→        )
   646→    output_file, elapsed = subprocess_artifacts
   647→
   648→    return _record_stage_report_if_needed(
   649→        context=context,
   650→        stage=stage,
   651→        handler=handler,
   652→        dependencies=dependencies,
   653→        output_file=output_file,
   654→        elapsed=elapsed,
   655→        stages_data=stages_data,
   656→    )
   657→
   658→
   659→def execute_stage(
   660→    context: StageRunContext,
   661→    *,
   662→    handlers: Mapping[str, StageHandler],
   663→    dependencies: StageExecutionDependencies,
   664→) -> StageExecutionResult:
   665→    """Execute one stage and return an explicit stage outcome."""
   666→    stage = context.stage
   667→    handler = handlers.get(stage)
   668→    prompt_mode = handler.prompt_mode if handler is not None else "output_only"
   669→
   670→    preflight_ok, preflight_reason = preflight_stage(
   671→        stage=stage,
   672→        prompt_mode=prompt_mode,
   673→        plan=context.plan,
   674→        triage_input=context.triage_input,
   675→        dry_run=context.dry_run,
   676→        append_run_log=context.append_run_log,
   677→        validate_reflect_issue_accounting=dependencies.validate_reflect_issue_accounting,
   678→    )
   679→    if not preflight_ok:
   680→        elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - context.stage_start)
   681→        return _failure_result(
   682→            stage=stage,
   683→            elapsed=elapsed,
   684→            error=preflight_reason or "stage_preflight_failed",
   685→            printed_message=f"  Stage {stage}: blocked before launch ({preflight_reason}).",
   686→        )
   687→
   688→    parallel_result = _execute_parallel_stage(
   689→        context=context,
   690→        stage=stage,
   691→        handler=handler,
   692→    )
   693→    if parallel_result.status != "ready":
   694→        return parallel_result
   695→    if parallel_result.used_parallel:
   696→        return StageExecutionResult(status="ready", payload={})
   697→    return _run_stage_subprocess_path(
   698→        context=context,
   699→        stage=stage,
   700→        handler=handler,
   701→        prompt_mode=prompt_mode,
   702→        dependencies=dependencies,
   703→    )
   704→
   705→
   706→__all__ = [
   707→    "DEFAULT_STAGE_HANDLERS",
   708→    "StageExecutionResult",
   709→    "StageExecutionDependencies",
   710→    "StageHandler",
   711→    "build_reflect_repair_prompt",
```

| 35 | `DepGraphBuilder = Callable[[Path], dict[str, dict[str, Any]]]` | **Lazily untyped** | Dependency graph values are language-agnostic adjacency structures; same shape everywhere. Inner dict values could be any detector/analysis output, but the structure is uniform. |

The "Any" represents detector-specific fields like `{file, line, summary, tier, confidence, detail}`. |

| 88 | `def runtime_setting(self, key: str, default: Any = None) -> Any` | **Genuinely polymorphic** | Settings values vary by setting name/type (bool, int, str, etc.); returns the appropriate type. |

**Summary:** Only `runtime_setting()` and `runtime_option()` are truly polymorphic by design (key-based lookup returns heterogeneous types). Everything else is lazily untyped but actually uniform in shape across languages.
