### 2. Full Content of `languages/_framework/base/shared_phases.py`

The file is at `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/_framework/base/shared_phases.py` (shown in full above, 99 lines).

**Key mechanism (lines 74–81):**

```python
def phase_security(path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
    """Compatibility wrapper with patchable security detector dependency."""
    original = shared_phases_review_mod.detect_security_issues
    shared_phases_review_mod.detect_security_issues = detect_security_issues
    try:
        return shared_phases_review_mod.phase_security(path, lang)
    finally:
        shared_phases_review_mod.detect_security_issues = original
```

**What this does:**
- Saves the original `detect_security_issues` bound in `shared_phases_review` module
- Swaps it with the module-level `detect_security_issues` from line 35 (initially set to the default from shared_phases_review)
- Calls the actual implementation in `shared_phases_review_mod.phase_security()`
- Restores the original in the finally block

**Why this wrapper exists:** It allows test code (and potentially language plugins) to patch `shared_phases_review_mod.detect_security_issues` by setting the module-level `detect_security_issues` variable in `shared_phases.py`, which then gets swapped in at call time. This decouples the phase function from the detector import.

---

### 3. `DetectorPhase` Type/Dataclass Definition

Located in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/_framework/base/types.py` lines 40–51:

```python
@dataclass
class DetectorPhase:
    """A single phase in the scan pipeline.

    Each phase runs one or more detectors and returns normalized issues.
    The `run` function handles both detection AND normalization (converting
    raw detector output to issues with tiers/confidence).
    """

    label: str
    run: Callable[[Path, LangRuntimeContract], tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, int]]]
    slow: bool = False
```

**Key points:**
- Not a Protocol, but a concrete @dataclass
- `run` is a callable that takes a path and runtime contract, returns issues + metadata dict
- `slow` flag marks phases that should run last (duplication detection)

---

### 4. `LangRuntimeContract` Protocol Definition

Located in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/_framework/base/types.py` lines 54–92:

```python
class LangRuntimeContract(Protocol):
    """Explicit runtime interface consumed by detector phases.

    This Protocol lives here (rather than in runtime.py) to avoid an import
    cycle: runtime.py imports concrete types from this module, and
    DetectorPhase.run references LangRuntimeContract in its signature.
    """

    name: str
    extensions: list[str]
    entry_patterns: list[str]
    barrel_names: set[str]
    external_test_dirs: list[str]
    test_file_extensions: list[str]
    review_low_value_pattern: object | None
    file_finder: FileFinder | None
    extract_functions: FunctionExtractor | None
    get_area: Callable[[str], str] | None
    build_dep_graph: DepGraphBuilder
    detect_lang_security_detailed: Callable[[list[str], FileZoneMap | None], LangSecurityResult]
    detect_private_imports: Callable[[dict, FileZoneMap | None], tuple[list[dict], int]]
    large_threshold: int
    complexity_threshold: int
    props_threshold: int

    zone_map: FileZoneMap | None
    dep_graph: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] | None
    complexity_map: dict[str, float]
    review_cache: dict[str, Any]
    review_max_age_days: int
    subjective_assessments: dict[str, Any]
    detector_coverage: dict[str, DetectorCoverageRecord]
    coverage_warnings: list[DetectorCoverageRecord]

    def runtime_setting(self, key: str, default: Any = None) -> Any: ...

    def runtime_option(self, key: str, default: Any = None) -> Any: ...

    def scan_coverage_prerequisites(self) -> list[DetectorCoverageStatus]: ...
```

**Key insight:** This is a Protocol (structural type), not a class. `LangConfig` implements it implicitly by having matching attributes and methods. Languages don't explicitly subclass it.

### 5. Who Calls `phase_security()` and How

**Direct callers:**

1. **Phase builders** (`phase_builders.py` lines 39–41):
   ```python

       "Security", phase_security,
   ```

   Creates a `DetectorPhase` with `phase_security` as the `run` function.

2. **Language configs** (e.g., `/languages/python/__init__.py` line 107):

   All ~13 language plugins call this factory to include the security phase.

3. **Generic registration** (`generic_support/registration.py` line 137):
   ```python

   ```
   Generic language scaffolding.

4. **Test mocks** (`tests/lang/common/test_shared_phases_security.py` line 31):
   ```python
   issues, potentials = phase_security(tmp_path, _lang_stub(files_scanned=7))
   ```
   Direct test invocation with mocked `detect_security_issues`.

**Execution flow:**
1. Phase is wrapped in `DetectorPhase("Security", phase_security, slow=False)`
2. Scanner loop iterates phases and calls `phase.run(path, lang_runtime)`
3. This invokes the `phase_security()` wrapper from `shared_phases.py`
4. Wrapper swaps in the (patchable) `detect_security_issues` function
5. Calls real `phase_security()` from `shared_phases_review.py`
6. Real implementation:
   - Calls `lang.file_finder(path)` to get file list
   - Calls `detect_security_issues(files, zone_map, lang.name, scan_root=path)` (cross-language detector)
   - Calls `lang.detect_lang_security_detailed(files, zone_map)` (language-specific hook)
   - Merges entries, filters by zone, records coverage, logs summary
   - Returns normalized Issue list + metadata dict

Located in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/_framework/base/types_shared.py` lines 51–57:

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LangSecurityResult:
    """Normalized return shape for language-specific security hooks."""

    entries: list[dict]

    coverage: DetectorCoverageStatus | None = None
```

**Used by:** `LangRuntimeContract.detect_lang_security_detailed()` return type. Allows language plugins to return security entries + scan count + optional coverage confidence metadata.

### Summary for Action Plan Notes

**Constraints & Assumptions:**

1. **`phase_security` is a pure wrapper** that swaps a patchable dependency. The real work is in `shared_phases_review.py:phase_security()`.

2. **Two security detectors:**
   - Cross-language: `desloppify.engine.detectors.security.detector.detect_security_issues(files, zone_map, lang_name, scan_root)`
   - Language-specific: `lang.detect_lang_security_detailed(files, zone_map)` → `LangSecurityResult`

3. **All 13 language plugins use the same phase** via `detector_phase_security()` factory. No language-specific overrides of the phase itself.

4. **No genuine polymorphism in detector output shapes**—`Any` usage is all lazy typing of structurally uniform detector dicts.

5. **Patchability is intentional**: The wrapper swapping lets tests and language-specific hooks (via the `detect_security_issues` module-level reassignment) customize behavior without modifying phase_builders or language configs.

6. **Coverage tracking** is built-in via optional `DetectorCoverageStatus` on `LangSecurityResult`. The phase records it in `lang.detector_coverage` dict.
